eṉṟu uḷam pūrittut tōḻikku urittāḷ. kuṭumpa voṟṟumikkuk kuḻantippēṟu vēṇṭum eṉavum, ip pēṟṟāl maṟumippēṟum kiṭṭum eṉavum nammuṉṉōr nampiya eḷiyaneṟi peṟappaṭum.
cēramāṉ peruñcēral irumpoṟi veṟṟitanta muracik kuḷippāṭṭum poruṭṭu veḷiyē eṭuttuc ceṉṟiruntāṉ. muracu vikkum kaṭṭil taṉiyē kiṭantatu. avvamayam vanta pulavar mōcikīraṉār naṭiyayarcciyāl vīrakkaṭṭilip paṭukkik kaṭṭil eṉa mayaṅki, ataṉmēl ēṟit tuyiṉṟār. muracuviḻāk koṇṭāṭit tirumpiya aracaṉ muracu tūṅkavēṇṭiya iṭattu mōci tūṅkak kaṇṭāṉ. maṟam vāḻum tūya iṭattu maṭivāḻak kaṇṭāṉ; atuvum veṟṟiviḻāk koṇṭāṭi varumpōtu kaṇṭāṉ; palar muṉṉiliyil kivāḷōṭu kaṇṭāṉ eṉṟāl, mōcikīraṉār veṭṭuṇpār. cettoḻivār eṉṟaṉṟō nām etirpārppōm. naṭantatu vēṟu. muracit tariyiṟ kiṭattik kivāḷit tūra eṟintu veṇcāmaraṅ koṇṭu taṉ kiyālē mella vīcip pulavar tūkkatti mēlum vaḷarttāṉ. neṭitu uṟaṅki viḻitta pulavar vāḷ vīca vēṇṭiya vēntaṉ viciṟi vīcuvatip pārttut taṉ pulami yellām koṭṭip pārāṭṭiṉār. avvulakam yārkkuk kiṭikkum? iv vulakattup pukaḻ uṭiyōrkkuttāṉ kiṭikkum. appukaḻ eppaṭi varum? itupōṉṟa ariya nalla toṇṭu ceytāṟṟāṉ varum. eṉṉik koṉṟālum uṉakkup paḻiyilli; atu ceyyātu. eṉ pulamiyip pōṟṟip pukaḻ koṇṭāy. Ú ï§‡d« brŒtj‰F¡ fhuz« v‹d? ivvulakattu nalla paṇiceytu immip pukaḻ koṇṭōrk kallatu, maṟumiyulakattu iruppu illi eṉṟu viḷaṅkikkoṇṭa neṟit teḷivutāṉ.5
mataṉuṭi muḻavuttōḷ ōccit taṇṇeṉa
vīci yōyē viyaliṭam kamaḻa
ivaṇici yuṭiyōrk kalla tavaṇatu
uyarnili yulakat tuṟiyu ḷiṉmi
viḷaṅkak kēṭṭa māṟukol
valampaṭu kuricilnī īṅkitu ceyalē.  	(puṟam. 50)
eṉṟu peruñcēral irumpoṟiyiṉ iciyip pāṭuṅkāl, tamiḻiṉattiṉ ulakiyal neṟiyip pulappaṭuttutal kāṇka.
tamiḻar kaṇkaṇṭa ulakatti matittaṉar; vāḻuṅ kālattup peṟum iṉpatti uvantaṉar; aḻikka voṇṇāk kātal eṉṉum pālāṟṟalum, pakivarip porutu kaḷikkum maṟavāṟṟalum, akam puṟam eṉṉum pāvakikku muṟiyē poruḷkaḷāyiṉa eṉavum, paḻan tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ innila nōkkiṉarē. makkaḷ aṟinta meyyāṉa vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷik kaṇṭāṅkup puṉivatiyē viḻintaṉar eṉavum, tamiḻ neñcam kāṇappaṭṭa iñ ñālatti nallateṉṟu karutiṟṟu eṉavum, tamiḻarkaḷ varalāṟṟiṉ pērāciriyar cīṉivācaṉār, tamiḻiṉattiṉ meyyiyali variyaṟuttu moḻivatu niṉiyattakum. 6 toltamiḻar tam kāla varalāṟṟiṉ pērāciriyar civarācaṉār tamiḻilakkiyat tōṟṟatti mūṉṟu kālappakutikaḷāka ārāyvar; iyaṟkik kālam eṉṟu caṅkakālattukkup peyariṭuvar; ak kālap pāṭṭukkaḷil ivvulaka nalvāḻvukku             vēṇṭukikaḷum ulakaviṉpaṅkaḷumē peritum pāṭap paṭṭuḷa eṉṟu teḷivippar.7 iccāṉṟukaḷāl ivvulakat tiyum īṇṭuc ceyyum viṉikaḷiyumē mutaṉmiyāka matitta tamiḻiṉattiṉ kōṭpāṭu viḷaṅkum.
akattiṇiyiṉ uripporuḷ
nam piṟappōṭu oṭṭiyatu, ulakap piṟappi aruḷuvatu, yāṇṭum parantatu, uṇarcciyuḷ valiyatu.8 aimpulaviṉpamum oruṅku taruvatu9, eṇṇam col ceyalellām iṉippatu etu? kātal, kātal, kātal. ik kātalē, iyalpāṉa pāluṇarcciyē - akattiṇi yilakkiyattiṉ pāṭaṟ poruḷām. ulakiyalil vāḻviyalik kaṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ taṉiyilakkiyattukku vēṟu evvuṇarcci pāṭuporuḷāka irukka muṭiyum? ivvuṇarccikkut tiṇiyum tuṟiyum vakuttut taṉiyilakkiyam kāṇum eṇṇam vēṟu evviṉattukkut tōṉṟa muṭiyum? kātalukku aṭutta ulakap pēruṇarcci maṟam ātaliṉ, ataṟkum puṟattiṇiyeṉa oru taṉiyilakkiyam tamiḻar kāṇpārāyiṉar.
kāmamum vīramum allatu ciṟanta vāḻkki yuṇarccikaḷ vēṟilli. ivviraṇṭiṟkum iraṇṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ kaṇṭa tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ muḻuttiṟam namakkup perumitam taruvatākum. ivviraṇṭuṇarcciyuḷ vīram oru pālārkkiṭiyē nikaḻvatu; kāmam pālmāṟi nikaḻvatākaliṉ, ōviyak kāṭcikkum kaṟpaṉit toṭuppukkum, cuvi mikutikkum, akattiṇi yilakkiyamē iṭaṅkoḷvatu eṉṟu pulavōrkaḷ pōṟṟiṉar. vīrattiṉum kātalukkut talimi nalkiṉar. maṟattip pāṭum muṭivil kātali iṇittuk kāṭṭiṉar. pattuppāṭṭil uḷḷa porunarāṟṟuppaṭi, ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi, malipaṭu kaṭām mutaliyavi puṟap pāṭṭukkaḷ āyiṉum, iṟutiyil kātal veṇpākkaḷ amintiruppati niṉika.
tamiḻukku oru taṉipperu mutal nūlāṉa tirukkuṟaḷ kāmattuppāleṉa ōriyal vakuttu aintiṇit tuṟi yaṉittiyum cevvipeṟap pāṭukiṉṟatu. ulakiṟku aṟaṅkariya vanta tiruvaḷḷuvar kāmakkūṟukaḷi akattiṇi neṟippaṭip pāṭuvāṉēṉ? tamiḻiṉattu aṟiviṉ imayattup piṟanta tūya pulaneṟi vaḻakkic collātuviṭutal, tamiḻp pulami peṟṟa tamakkum, tamiḻil eḻutappaṭum tam vāḻvu nūlukkum iḻukkām eṉṟu karutiyiruppar. mēlum ik kātalneṟi naṉṟeṉat terintiruntum, makkaṭku aṟamāka uriyāviṭil, ulakiṟkuc ceyta vañcakamām eṉṟum karutiyiruppar. tamiḻ moḻikkaṇ tōṉṟiya camaya vilakkiyaṅkaḷum, aṟavilakkiyaṅkaḷum poruḷālum, vaṭivālum akattiṇikkuṭpaṭṭa kaṭaṉ collun taramaṉṟu. kaṭavuṭ kātalip pāṭiyaruḷiya āḻvār nāyaṉmārkaḷ makkaṭ kātalukkeṉa vakutta akattiṇi yulakac cuvaṭukaḷip piṉpaṟṟiyatālaṉṟō - teyva aṉpu aka ilakkiyat taṭattup pāyntatālaṉṟō - tamiḻakattil iṟip pācuraṅkaḷ putumiyum taṉimiyum peṟṟa ōr ilakkiyavulakamāka viḷaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.10 teyvap pulavarkaḷukku akattiṇip pulami illātiruntirukkumēl, ip pācuraṅkaḷiṉ ilakkiyat taram mikak kuṟivupaṭum. ilakkiyamō eṉṟa aiyattiṟkum uriyatāy viṭum. 

III
1. camutāya aṭippaṭi 
akattiṇi yilakkiyattiṉ uripporuḷāṉa kātal tamiḻiṉam koṇṭoḻukiya ulakiyal nōkkattukku muṟṟum iyintatu eṉṟu mēliyārāycciyiṟ kaṇṭōm. kātal taṉṉērillā valiya periya uṇarcciyē yāyiṉum, aḵtu uruppeṟṟum uṟuppuppeṟṟum ōr ilakkiyap paṭippu eytutaṟkut tuṇik kūṟukaḷ ettuṇiyō vēṇṭum. ik kūṟukaḷum kātaleṉṉum mūlapporuḷukkēṟpa ulakiṉavāka iruttal vēṇṭum. ilakkiyam eṉpatu makkaḷ vāḻvil kaṇṭavi nukarntavi eṇṇiyavi uṇarntavi tammip pulappaṭuttum pativēṭu eṉṟum, moḻivaḻippaṭṭa vāḻkki viḷakkam eṉṟum, āṅkilat tiṟaṉāḷi aṭcaṉār ilakkiya ārāycci muṉṉuri nūlil eḻutiyuḷḷār.11 ilakkiyam vāḻviliruntu nēraṭiyākat tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟāl, oru taṉi maṉitaṉ vāḻviliruntu tōṉṟuvatu eṉṟā poruḷ? makkaḷ palarkūṭi vāḻum camutāyak kūṭṭuṟaviliruntu ilakkiyam mukiḻkkum eṉṟu koḷka. camutāyap peruṅkaḷam iṉṟēl, makkaṭku viṉiyāṟṟalum nākarikap pāṅkukaḷum tōṉṟā. vēṟupaṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷ, vēṭkikaḷ, etirpārppukaḷ, nampikkikaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷellām camutāya nilik kaṇṇāṭiyiṟṟāṉ muḻutum kāṇappaṭum. aracu matam oḻukkappāṭellām camutāya nilipēṟṟukkut tāṉē vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ tamiḻar kaṇṭa akavilakkiyak kaṭṭiṭattukku vēṇṭum tuṇikkūṟukaḷum tamiḻc camutāyattiliruntē kiṭittaṉa eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. īrāyiram āṇṭukku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻiṉac camutāya vāḻvic caṅkap pāṭalkaḷē kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāṭṭavallaṉa.12 ippāṭalkaḷuḷ nāṉkil mum maṭaṅku akattiṇi cārntavi eṉiṉ, camutāyac ceytikaḷ akavilakkiyap paṭippil peṟṟuḷḷa pēriṭam veḷippaṭi.
kaḷavu neṟi
kaḷavoḻukkam paṇṭit tamiḻakattil paraviyiruntatu. peṟṟōr aṟiyāmal kātalarkaḷit tēṭikkoḷḷa muyaṉṟa iḷam pālārkaḷ aṉṟu mikappalar. iravum pakalum kaḷavuk kūṭṭaṅkaḷ nikaḻntaṉa eṉavum, tērēṟiyum vantu ik kaḷavu naṭattum tuṇivu iruntatu eṉavum aṟikiṉṟōm. ivvūril naḷḷiravil evvaḷavō tērkaḷ vantu celkiṉṟaṉa. eṉṉip pōṉṟa iḷamakaḷir palar ivvūril vāḻkiṉṟārkaḷ. tuyar ceyyāta aṉput tāyip peṟṟiruppatu avarkaḷiṉ nalla kālam. ōr tēr vantu pōyiṟṟu eṉṟu yārō collak kēṭṭa eṉ aṉṉi aiyappaṭṭu eṉṉit tollippaṭuttu kiṉṟāḷ.
paṉikkaḻi tuḻavum pāṉāḷ taṉittōr
tērvantu peyarnta teṉpa ataṟkoṇṭu
ōrum alikkum aṉṉi piṟarum
piṉṉuviṭu katuppiṉ miṉṉiḻi makaḷir
iḷiyarum maṭavarum uḷarē
aliyāt tāyaroṭu naṟpā lōrē. 	(kuṟun. 246)
eṉṟu ōr iḷanaṅki koḷḷum poṟāmiyilum, ūril kaḷavu mikutiyum naṭantatu eṉṟu aṟiyalām. caṅkakālattil variyātu uṭaṉpōyiṉār palar eṉṟāl, kaḷavoḻukkam oḻukiṉār palappalarātal vēṇṭumaṉṟē. maṟṟum oru cāṉṟu; makaḷ uṭaṉpōṉapiṉ, cevili tēṭip puṟappaṭṭāḷ. pāli nilattiṉ vaḻineṭukac ceṉṟāḷ; naṭantu naṭantu mutiya kālkaḷum taḷḷāṭiṉa. āṇum peṇṇum iruvar iruvarākat tūrattē varakkaṇṭāḷ. tammavarō eṉṟu āciyoṭu ovvoru muṟiyum pārttāḷ. vēṟu iḷiyavaṉum iḷiyavaḷumāka irukkakkaṇṭu avalittāḷ. kātalarāka etirē varuvār palariruntum, vēṟṟuvarāka uḷḷaṉarē eṉṟu ēṅkiṉāḷ; uṟṟup pārttup pārttu avaḷ kaṇkaḷ oḷi maḻuṅkiṉa.
kālē paritap piṉavē kaṇṇē
nōkki nōkki vāḷiḻan taṉavē
akaliru vicumpiṉ mīṉiṉum
palarē maṉṟaviv vulakattup piṟarē13  	(kuṟun. 44)
uṭaṉpōkum kātaṟcōṭikaḷ vāṉattu mīṉkaḷiṉum palarāvar eṉṟa uvamiyāl, paṇṭit tamiḻakattil kaḷaviṉ perukkam nilaviṟṟu eṉpatu aṅkinelli. 
camutāya icivu
iṉpattuṟiyil avaravar viruppattukku iṭaṉuṇṭu eṉavum, oruvar viruppatti veḷippaṭuttaliṉ kaḷavuneṟi naṉṉeṟiyē eṉavum tamiḻc camutāyam oppiyatōṭu, anneṟikku ātaravum aḷittatu. kātaṟkāmam kāmattuc ciṟantatu (pari. 9) eṉṟu kuṉṟampūtaṉār kaḷavoḻukkattiṉ perumiyi eṭutturippar. āṇum peṇṇum viruppam ottukkūṭum kūṭṭam (viruppu ōrottatu meyyuṟu puṇarcci) eṉṟu ataṟku ētukkāṭṭuvar. kaḷavu neṟikku icinta nam camutāyam anneṟi vaḷara vēṇṭiya cūḻnili yiyum vakuttu utaviṟṟu. iḷiñarkaḷ oṉṟukuḻumutaṟkum, avartam tīraṅkaḷi iḷiñiyar muṉ amarntu kāṇpataṟkum ēṟṟa maṟaviḻākkaḷ pala nikaḻntaṉa. ēṟukōḷviḻā mulli nilattup paṇṭu koṇṭāṭiya peruviḻāvākum. iḵtu āṇṭu tōṟum yāṇṭum nikaḻum. ivviḻāvil mullik kumariyarkaḷ uṭaṉ vaḷartta kāḷiyēṟukaḷ viraṭṭappaṭum. koṉṟiyum kāyāvum veṭciyum piṭavum taḷavum cūṭi mullik kātalarkaḷ ēṟupiṭikkap puṟappaṭuvar. mulli varici pōṉṟa pallum kuḷirnta kaṇṇum pētic collum poṟṟōṭum koṇṭa aḻakiyar paraṇmēl vīṟṟiruntu āravārañceytu viḻāviṟku uṇarcciyūṭṭuvar. iṅṅaṉam kāḷiyar ōṭuṅkāḷiyic cāṭip piṭikkumpōtu, kumariyar kūṭiyiruntu kāṇum vāyppiṉip paṇṭic camutāyam nalkiṟṟu.
pallāṉ potuvar kataḻviṭi kōṭkāṇmār
mulli mukiyum muruntum nirittaṉṉa
pallar perumaḻik kaṇṇar maṭañcērnta
collar cuṭaruṅ kaṉaṅkuḻik kātiṉar
nallavar koṇṭār viṭi.   	(kali. 103)
mintarum makaḷirum viruppampōl palarkāṇat taḻuvik kikōttāṭum tuṇaṅkik kūttu muṉpu peruvaḻakkāka iruntatu. maḷḷar kuḻīiya viḻavu, makaḷir taḻīiya tuṇaṅki eṉpatu kuṟuntoki (31). āṭuvār iṟukattaḻuvi āṭuvatu ik kūttiṉ iyalpātaliṉ taḻūu eṉṟa oru peyar (tuṇaṅkiyañ cīrttaḻūu maturik kāñci. 160) itaṟku ēṟpaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṇṇiṟanta viḻākkaḷum āṭalkaḷum pāṭalkaḷum varikaḷum tamiḻakattup polinta ēṟṟattic caṅkap paṉuvalkaḷāl aṟikiṉṟōm. iviyellām irupālārum aṭikkaṭi neruṅkip paḻakutaṟkum, paḻakkam kaḷavoḻukkamāka vaḷartaṟkum vaḻiceytaṉa. perutta maḻiyil paṭṭamaram taḷirttāṟpōla, mutiyōrkkum kātaluṇarvu maḻuṅkātiruntatu. ciṟāṉum, ciṟāḷumkūṭi viḷiyāṭit tiriyum kuḻanti naṭpiṉi aṟṟic camutāyam taṭukkavilli kāṇ.
ceyvuṟu pāvikkuk koypūt tiit
taṇkayam āṭum makaḷiroṭu kipiṇintu
taḻuvuvaḻit taḻīit tūṅkuvaḻit tūṅki
maṟiyeṉal aṟiyā māyamil āyam	(puṟam. 243)
eṉṟum pulavar toṭittali viḻuttaṇṭiṉār ciṟuvar kūṭṭamum ciṟumiyar kūṭṭamum kipiṇintu meytaḻuvi viḷiyāṭum kuḻantit tuṇaṅkiyik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. ivaḷum ivaṉum ciṟṟil iḻittum ciṟupaṟiyaṟintum viḷiyāṭupava eṉṟu ilakkaṇarkaḷ kāṭṭum naṭiyil mēlit tamiḻc camutāya vaḻakkam pulappaṭutal kāṇka.
ūrppeṇkaḷ iḷaṅ kātalarkaḷiṉ maṟiyoḻuk katti aṟavē kaṭintu otukkār. avartam pōkkiṟku iṭiyūṟāy niṉṟu vilakkār, vilakkiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu colla yātoru cāṉṟum illi. kātalarkaḷiṉ varavu celavukaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu makiḻntu iṉpuṟuvatu, iṉṉavaḷukku iṉṉavaṉōṭu toṭarpu eṉṟu palaraṟiyap pēcip parappuvatu, iviyē alarvāyp peṇkaḷiṉ vēliyākum. ūrār tuṟivaṟku eṉṉip peṇṭeṉa moḻipa (aiṅ. 112) eṉṟu oru talivi ūr nōkkattic cuṭṭikkāṭṭuvaḷ. alar kaḷavu neṟikku eṉṟum pakiyāka vaḷarntatilli. kātalōrkaḷip pirittu uṟavik keṭukka muyaṉṟatilli. peṇṇiṉ uṟṟārkkum ūrār piṟarkkum nammūrkkaṇ kaḷavoṉṟu naṭakkiṉṟatu eṉpataṉi ampalappaṭuttum naṉṉōkkuṭi yatē alarāvatu (akam. 40). num makaḷ virumpum nampi ivaṉē. iṉi maṇaṅkāṇka eṉṟu alar peṟṟōrkkuk kalakkamiṉṟi vaḻikāṭṭukiṉṟatu. tam makaḷ tērnta ammakaṉ peṟṟōrkkup piṭikkātiruntālum, eṉ ceyvatu, ūraṟinta uṟaviṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭuttāṉē ākavēṇṭum. eṉavē peṟṟōr tirumaṇattiṟku muṟiyāṉa ēṟpāṭu ceykuvar. ceyyavē, kaḷavik kaṟpāṟṟuppaṭuttiya alarum, ōṭum neñcu oru noṭiyil niṉṟatu pōla, tāṉē kūmpi yoḻiyak kāṇkiṉṟōm. alarvāyp peṇṭir naṅkiyi maṇappaṭuttit tammoṭu oruttiyākak kūṭṭik koṇṭapiṉ, avarukkup paṟṟukkōṭu uṇṭō?
ampalum alarum tāṅkamāṭṭātu añciya iḷaṅkumari ivvalarik kāṭṭilum pāli veyil koṭumiyāmō? tāṅkattakka iṉiyataṉṟē eṉṟu koḻunaṉōṭu curam pōtalum uṇṭu. appōtu, alar poḻinta ariviyarkaḷ iṉiya poruḷip paṟikoṭuttāṟ pōla varuntavum iraṅkavum kāṇkiṉṟōm. maṉi maruṇṭirunta eṉṉiṉum....... ūr iḻantaṉṟu taṉvīḻvuṟu poruḷē (akam. 189). ivvāṟu taṉ kavaliyiṉum ūrkkavali peritāyiṟṟu eṉat tāy pulampukiṉṟāḷ. itaṉāl ūralar aṉpuvaḻippaṭṭatu eṉpatu peṟappaṭum.
tamiḻc camutāyam kaḷavumuṟiyi oruneṟiyākak kaṇṭu ōmpiyatu eṉṟa karuttu iṉṉum valiyuṟak kāṇpōm. uṭaṉpōya kātalarkaḷ iṭiccurattu veyilukku āṟṟātu marāmarattiṉ niḻalil taṅkiṉar; nīr viṭāykku nellikkaṉi kaḷit tiṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉar. ivarkaḷ ennāṭṭiṉarō, evvūriṉarō, evarmakkaḷō? yārāyiṉum aruḷukkum ātaravukkum uriyavarkaḷ. ēṉ? iṇipiriyā makaṉṟiṟ paṟavi pōla eṉṟum nīṅkātu uṭaṉirukkum kātaṟ koḷki yuṭiyavarkaḷ;  
yārkol aḷiyartāmē vārciṟik
kuṟuṅkāl makaṉṟil aṉṉa
uṭaṉpuṇar koḷkik kāta lōrē	(aiṅ. 381)
eṉṟu ivviruvariyum kaṇṭa vaḻippōkkiṉar pārāṭṭu vārāyiṉar. uṭaṉpuṇar koḷkik kātalōr eṉṟa toṭarāl, kaṟpukkaṭaṉ pūṇṭa ivvuṭaṉpōkku vaḻip pōkunarkkum uṭaṉpāṭē eṉpatu peṟappaṭum. uṭaṉ pōya makaḷit tēṭicceṉṟāḷ oru cevilittāy. taṉ makaḷiyum avaḷ kātalaṉiyum kaṇṭatuṇṭō eṉṟu etirppaṭṭa mukkōṟpakavarit tuṭituṭittu viṉaviṉāḷ. tamiḻiyal aṟinta antaṇāḷarkaḷ, kaṇṭōm kaṇṭōm. kaṟpuvaḻip paṭṭāḷic curattiṭik kaṇṭōm. avaḷ ceṉṟa neṟiyē taliyāya aṟaneṟi. vāḻvukkuc ciṟantāṉi vaḻippaṭṭuc ceṉṟa kaṟpiyiṉ poruṭṭu, tāyē, kavalaṟka eṉṟu aṟamum amitiyum tōṉṟa ētukkāṭṭiṉar (kali. 6). uṭaṉ puṇarikaḷi vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭār yārum. ivvakip pōkkut tavaṟu, peṟṟōr aṟiyā iccelavu paḻiyuṭiyatu eṉa maṟittu niṟuttiyatāka oru pāṭaṟ kuṟippum illi. ik kātaṟ celavi varavēṟṟa pāṭalkaḷē uḷa. itaṉāl naṉmik kātalarkaḷ maṟivoḻukkattiṟ peṟṟa potu aṉpum aḷiyum camutāya icivum teriya varum.
kuṭumpa icivu
maṟivuc ceytiyik kēṭṭatum, taṭam piṟaḻntāṟ pōlat tuṭikkum cilar uṇṭu. avar talimakaḷiṉ kuṭumpat tārāvar. peṟṟōrkaḷ kaḷavu neṟiyi uṭaṉ paṭātu katappaṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu koḷḷak kūṭātu.14 aṉṉōr ciṉappu kaḷavu neṟi mēlaṉṟu; anneṟippaṭṭa makaḷ mēlām. nāmirukka, maṇañceytuvippatu nam kaṭamiyāyiruppa, kaṭamiyi uṇarum nammi maṟantu, makaḷ tāṉē taṉakkoruvaṉit tēṭalāmā? im mutaleṇṇam ep peṟṟōkkum eḻavē ceyyum. camutāya muṉṉiliyil kuṭimāṉattiṟku iḻukkeṉavē tāy tantiyar tuṭippaṭivar. ittuṭippu kaṭami yuṇarcciyil eḻuvatēyaṉṟik kaḷavu veṟuppil eḻuvataṉṟu kāṇ. taṉakkup piṭittavaṉi makaḷ maṇappatilō avaḷi maṇappaṭuttuvatilō peṟṟōr taṭiyāka nillār. tamakkup piṭittavaṉittāṉ tammakaḷ piṭittavaṉākkik koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟu vaṉmam ceyyār. iḵtu akattiṇi ilakkiyam kāṭṭum peṟṟōr iyalpu. eṉiṉum kaṉṟu tāṉē aviḻttuk koṇṭu pāl kuṭippati māṭṭāḷi virumpuvatuṇṭō? pārāṭṭic cīrāṭṭip paruvamvari vaḷartta makaḷiṉ maṉappōkkē - urimi yuṇarcciyē - peṟṟōrkkuc ciṟiya maṉakkacappi ūṭṭa vallatākum. maṉaṅkacappiṉum, kātaṟkaḷam terintapiṉ, makaḷ viruppattiṟku māṟu cellum peṟṟōrilar. vēṟāka oruvaṉi maṇamakaṉāka vitikkum peṟṟōrilar. atupōla makaḷ ceytiyik kaṇṭatum kēṭṭatum. ām, naṉku ceytāḷ, vallavaḷ eṉṟu mukamalarntu vāymoḻiyum peṟṟōrumilar.
avarum,
terikaṇi nōkkic cilinōkkik kaṇcēntu
orupaka lellām urutteḻun tāṟi
iruvarkaṭ kuṟṟamum illiyāl eṉṟu
terumantu cāyttār tali.	(kali. 39)
maṉitavuṇarcciyum tamiḻ nākarikamum piṉṉiya pāṭṭu itu. taṅkiyiṉ kaḷavuppuṇarcciyit tāy collāmaṟ collak kēṭṭa aṇṇaṉmārkaḷ kuṭivaṭuppaṭa ippaṭiyum naṭantatu kol? naṭakkat tūṇṭiya avvāṭavaṉ yār? oruki pārppōm eṉṟu kotittaṉar. kūriya ampiṉiyum koṭiya villiyum vīrattāl nōkkiṉar. itu takātu eṉṟu orukāl vīṟiṭṭu eḻuntaṉar. viḷivu eṉṉāmō eṉṟu maṟukāl amintaṉar. ivvuḷḷap pūcal cila poḻutuvari niṉṟatu. ivaḷukku oruvaṉ, avaṉukku orutti vāḻvukku vēṇṭum; taṉ vāḻvukkut takkārit tāṉē tēṭi eytuvatil piḻipāṭu eṉṉō? yārpālum kuṟṟam illi eṉṟu teḷintaṉar. teḷiyavē, kātalarip pirittataṟkup puṇaravirunta ampum villum kūṭātoḻintaṉa. pōrkkaḷak karuvikaḷit taṅki yiṉ kātaṟkaḷattil payaṉpaṭutta eṭuttōmē? kātalukku vīram tuṇiyāka vēṇṭum; eṉceytōm? eṉṟu aṇṇaṉmār nāṇattāl talikīḻāyiṉar. kātaṟ kaḷavu tamiḻnilattil aṟamākap pōṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉṟu itaṉālum uṟutippaṭum.
kaḷavum kaṟpē
tamiḻ varalāṟu eṉṉum nūlāciriyar kaḷavu aṟamākātu; piḻi neṟiyām eṉṟu viritturikkak kāṇkiṟōm. caṅkap pāṭalkaḷukku ivar piḻiyākac ceytu koṇṭa karutturikaḷ ippiḻi muṭipiṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu kāṭṭuvaṉ:
kuṟamakaḷ avaḷem kulamakaḷ avaḷoṭum
aṟumuka voruvaṉiṉ aṭiyiṇi toḻutēm
tuṟimici niṉatiru tiruvaṭi toṭunar
peṟukanaṉ maṇamviṭu piḻimaṇam eṉavē	(cilap. kuṉṟa. 18)
piḻi maṇatti oḻittu naṉ maṇatti aruḷuka eṉṟu tōḻiyum taliviyum murukaṉpāl vēṇṭukiṉṟaṉar eṉa iḷaṅkō kūṟuvar. piḻi maṇam eṉpatu kaḷavik kuṟikkum   eṉṟum kaḷavoḻukkattit tavirttu nāṭaṟinta naṉmaṇa mākiya kaṟpoḻukkatti muṭippikka eṉpatē tōḻiyiṉ vēṇṭuri eṉṟum, kaḷavip piḻimaṇam eṉṟataṉāl kaḷavu tītu eṉpatu teḷivu eṉṟum, makāvittuvāṉ rā. irākavaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ muṭivukku varuvar. iḷaṅkō piḻimaṇam eṉṟatu kaḷaviyā? illi; illi. akat tiṇik kaḷavil veṟiyāṭṭu nikaḻum eṉa muṉṉē paṭittiruk kiṉṟōm. ivaḷ vāṭṭam murukaṉāl viḷintatu eṉṟu pūcāri vaḻakkappaṭi colvāṉ. avaṉi ikaḻvatum, avaṉ mēl ēṟiya muruka teyvatti ikaḻvatum kātaliyartam tuṇiccal. 
vēlaṉ maṭavaṉ avaṉiṉum tāṉmaṭavaṉ
ālamar celvaṉ putalvaṉ varumāyiṉ	     (cilap. kuṉṟa. 13)
eṉac cilappatikārattuk kuṟiñcik kātalāḷiṉ tuṇivuriyik kēṭkiṉṟōm. ivveṟiyāṭṭamayattu varuvatu peṟuka naṉmaṇam viṭu piḻimaṇam eṉṟa aṭi. eṉ kātalāṉ iṉṉum kaḷaviyē virumpukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉukku ak kaḷaveṇṇattit tavirttaruḷ; tirumaṇa eṇṇatti ūṭṭuka eṉṟu talivaṉ ceyalic collutaṟkāka veṟiyāṭṭu naṭappatilli. talivaṉit tiruttuka eṉṟu murukaṉ muṉ muṟiyiṭār taliviyar. talivaṉatu neṟiyip paṟṟip pēcum iṭam veṟiyiṭam aṉṟu eṉṟa marapi nām uṇara vēṇṭum. veṟiyum notumalar varivum kaṟpukku ēlā. ātaliṉ, avaṟṟi vilakka muyalvar kātaṟ peṇṭukaḷ eṉṟu muṉṉarē viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. am muyaṟciyic cilappatikārap pāṭalkaḷum pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. vēlaṉ vantu veṟiyāṭum kaḷattu mayilmēl murukaṉ varuvāṉ; vantāl. malināṭaṉatu tirumaṇatti vēṇṭuvōm (kuṉṟa. 15). (vēṟu maṇatti vēṇṭōm eṉpatu kuṟippu). ayal maṇam oḻi, avar maṇatti aruḷeṉat toḻuvōm (. 16); avar palaraṟimaṇam ceytu koḷvār eṉak kūppuvōm (. 17). iṅṅaṉamākat tōḻiyum taliviyum ayalmaṇa voḻi viyum avar maṇavuṟutiyiyum cuṭṭi moḻintu varukāli, naṉmaṇam peṟuka. piḻimaṇam viṭuka (. 18) eṉṟu kūṟimuṭippar. ātaliṉ piḻi maṇam eṉpatu notumalar varivi - talivaṉ allāta piṟaṉukku maṇampēcuvatik kuṟippatākum. piṟar maṇamē kaṟpukkuk kēṭu payakkum tavaṟāṉa maṇamākum; aṟaṅ kaṭanta maṇamākum. ātaliṉaṉṟē aṟattoṭu niṟṟal vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉpaṉa camutāyanili nōkkiya coṟkaḷ.  camutāyattiṟku maṟivu kaḷavu eṉavum, veḷippaṭi kaṟpu eṉavum paṭum. uḷḷam oṉṟiya kātalartam aṉpunili nōkkiṉ, kaḷavum kaṟpēyām. camutāya oḻuṅkukku uṭpaṭu vāṉ tūyakātalarkaḷ maṇanili koḷvar. aḵtaṉṟit tirumaṇattāl uḷḷam oṉṟum eṉṟō, kātal tūymi peṟum eṉṟō akattiṇikkaṇ poruḷkoḷḷal ākātu. kātalarkaḷ eññāṉṟum kaṟpuṭiyavarkaḷ, aṉpiṉar eṉpatu akattiṇik kōḷākum. itu tīmi payakkum kaḷavaṉmi koḷka15 eṉpar iḷampūraṇar (tol. poruḷ. 487). iḷiyōḷ vaḻuvilaḷ eṉpatu oru tāy moḻi (naṟ. 143):
curanaṉi iṉiya tākuka tilla
aṟaneṟi ituveṉat teḷintaveṉ
piṟinutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ pōkiya curaṉē 	(aiṅ. 371)
kaḷavoḻuki uṭaṉpōkiya taṉmakaḷ aṟaneṟi teḷintavaḷ eṉṟu tāy teḷiyak kāṇkiṉṟōm. ātaliṉ, akattiṇi kūṟum kaḷavu aṟaneṟippaṭṭatu eṉṟu tuṇivōmāka. piḻi maṇam - ayalār maṇam eṉpatu nāṭṭāriṉ nalluri. aṟañcālum akattiṇik kaḷavu pulavar tam paṭippaṉṟu; tamiḻc camutāyattil paravik kiṭanta oḻukkamākum. makkaḷ vāḻkkiyil ūṟikkalanta iṉpak kaḷavuṇarcciyi akattiṇip paṭippiṟku naṟkūṟākap pulavarkaḷ taḻīik koṇṭaṉar.

IV
marapuneṟi
kaḷavukkum kaṟpukkum uriya toṭarpu eṉṉa? itu mikavum ārāya vēṇṭuvatu. kaṟpāvatu nalvāḻvu eṉṟum, kaḷavāvatu avvāḻvi eytuvikkum oru naṉṉeṟiyē eṉṟum koḷka.16
māmayi laṉṉār maṟiyiṟ puṇarmintar
kāmam kaḷaviṭṭuk kikoḷ kaṟpuṟṟeṉa           (pari. 11)
ivvāṟu iraṇṭaṉ toṭarpi āciriyar nallantuvaṉār eṭuttukkāṭṭuvar. kaḷavu koṇṭuviṭutaṟkuriyatu; kaṟpu kikkoḷḷutaṟ kuriyatu eṉṟu kaḷaviṉ niliyāmiyiyum kaṟpiṉ nilimiyiyum uṟavupaṭuttik kūṟuvar. itaṉāl kaṟpu vāḻkkikkuk kaḷavu nalla tōṟṟuvāy eṉappaṭumaṉṟi, it tōṟṟuvāyiṉṟik kaṟpiyal amiyātu. ciṟavātu eṉappaṭātu. nalla kātalvāḻkki kaḷavāṉē mukiḻkkum eṉṟal poruḷaṉṟu. kaṟpeṉappaṭuvatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻittē eṉpatu iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal (15). ikkaruttu akattamiḻ neṟiyaṉṟu. ovvoru kaṟpiyalukku muṉṉum kaḷaviyal nikaḻntātal vēṇṭum eṉavum, tirumaṇaṅ kūṭṭum orē neṟi, kaḷavutāṉ eṉavum kūṟuvār kūṟṟu altamiḻ eṉa akaṟṟuka. iṟiyaṉār yātta ivvilakkaṇam akavilakkiyattilum tamiḻ maṉpatiyiṉ vāḻkkiyilum kāṇāta kaṭṭuriyākum. pettaraṇ racal eḻutukiṟār; maruṭkik kātal vāḻvil āḻntakaṉṟa iṉpakkaḷippi ūṭṭum eṉṟu karutukiṉṟēṉ. oruvaṉum oruttiyum vēṭki, vēṭpu, meṉmiyōṭu kātaṟ paṭuvarēl, akkātal iṉitteṉa aṟiyā nayamuṭiyatu. ik kātal nayap pāṅki maṉitaviṉam uṇaravēṇṭum. uṇarāmi aṟiyāmiyākum. kaḷavukkātal vāḻviṉ nōkkam ākā teṉiṉum, vāḻviṉ oru kūṟēyāmāyiṉum, kaḷaviṉāl varum ārā iṉpatti nukarntu tiḷikkac camutāya amippu kaṭṭāyam iṭaṅkoṭukka vēṇṭum eṉṟu eṇṇukiṉṟēṉ. iṅṅaṉam iruvar tammuṭ pullip peṟum taṉi iṉpattiṉum, illaṟam eṉpatu oruperum karuttuṭiyatu. aḵtu ōr oḻuṅkiyal, kuḻantikaḷip peṟṟu aḷippatāl, tirumaṇam camutāya vamippiṉ oru potuk kūṟākavum, kaṇavaṉ maṉivi iruvariṉ conta nilikku appāṟpaṭṭa ciṟappuk kūṟākavum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ārvak kātal tirumaṇattiṟku oru tūṇṭukōlāka amiyavēṇṭum. amivatu naṉṟu eṉpatē eṉ karuttāyiṉum, oṉṟu colluvaṉ. kātalārvam kaṭici vari nillātu. makkaṭ koṭi eṉṉum camutāyap paṇi puriyātu. iṉpattōṭu camutāyak kaṭaṉāṟṟum varivuk kātal uṇṭē, atu mikka piṇippum aṉpum uṇmiyum uṭiyatu.17 ivvāṟu camutāyattil kaḷaviṟkum kaṟpiṟkum uriya iṭa nilikaḷi racalār ārāyntu vakuppar. ivar karuttōṭu tamiḻc camutāya amippi ottiṭuka. nam camutāyam kaḷavoḻukkatti oppiyatu, ūkkiyatu; āyiṉum kaḷavuk kātalē mutaṉmik kātal eṉṟu talimiyaḷikka villi. tirumaṇattiṟkuk kaḷavē vāyil eṉṟu orē vaḻi kāṭṭa villi.
kaḷavuneṟiyum marapuneṟiyum
tirumaṇa vāḻvu eṉṉum kaṟpiyalukku iruneṟikaḷ uḷa. kaḷavuneṟi, marapuneṟi eṉṟu avi collappaṭum. maṟi veḷippaṭutalum tamariṟpeṟutalum eṉpar tolkāppiyar (1444). ivviru neṟikaḷum kaḷavē caṅka vilakkiyattup pāṭal cāṉṟatu eṉpati nām aṟivōm. kaḷavoḻukkattil kātal neñcaṅkaḷ vimmi yeḻukiṉṟaṉa, virintu alikiṉṟaṉa, iyalpukaṭantu eṇṇuvatum caṅka vilakkiyattup pāṭal cāṉṟatu eṉpati nām aṟivōm. kaḷavoḻukkattil kātal neñcaṅkaḷ vimmi yeḻukiṉṟaṉa, virintu alikiṉṟaṉa, iyalpukaṭantu eṇṇuvatum colluvatum ceykiṉṟaṉa. ivveṇṇak kiḷarccikaḷ pāṭalukku viṟuviṟuppik koṭuttaliṉ, kaḷavu cāṉṟōrkaḷāl pāṭupeṟṟatu. aṉṉōr pāṭiyatu kaḷḷakkaḷavaṉṟu, kaṟpuṭiya kātaṟ kaḷavātaliṉ, kaḷavup pāṭal kālam kaṭanta ilakkiyamāy ilakkaṇam peṟṟatu. kaḷavuk kātal pāṭal koṇṭamiyāl anneṟi uyarntatu eṉṟō, peṟṟōr kūṭṭuvikkum iyalpuk kātal pāṭal peṟāmiyāl, inneṟi tāḻntatu eṉṟō koḷḷavē koḷḷaṟka. 
marapumuṟi akattiṇikkaṇ oru tuṟiyākap pēcap paṭutalilli. ilakkiyap paṭuttaṟku vēṇṭum. uṇarccik kūṟu ataṟku illi eṉṟukūṭak kūṟalām. tirumaṇamākiya ōrāṇṭiṟkuḷ āṇkuḻantip pēṟu kiṭittatu eṉṟu vittuk koḷvōm. ippēṟu ulakiṭip palarkku niṉippiṉṟi eḷitiṟ kiṭṭap pārkkiṟōm. iḵtōr ulakiyal. āṇṭuk kaṇakkāka etirpārttup pārttu nāḷellām kōyilkuḷam cuṟṟit talañceṉṟu toḻutu nīrāṭi, peṇṇāyiṉum oru kuḻantip pēṟṟukkut tavaṅ kiṭakkum illaṟa mutiyarkaḷum palar uḷar. eṇṇiyāṅkuk kuḻantic celvam eytiya ivarkaḷ kaṭavuḷ aruḷāṟ piṟantatu eṉṟum, muṉṉōr tavattāṟ piṟantatu eṉṟum koṇṭāṭukiṉṟaṉar. ikkoṇṭāṭṭattil uṟaviṉarum ūrārum kalantu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. iḵtōr ulakiyal, nāṭ kaṭantu utitta tavakkuḻanti ilakkiyat toṭuppiṟku nayap poruḷākalām. nāḷiṟ piṟanta kālak kuḻantimēl pāṭutaṟkuk karuttu vaṟṟalām.18 ilakkiyat toṭikkuk karuttuc curattalāl, tavakkuḻanti kālak kuḻantiyiṉum ēṟṟamuṭittu eṉṟō, nāṭkaṭantu peṟum makappēṟē ciṟantatu eṉṟō yārum collār. illaṟattiṟkuk kuḻavippēṟu vēṇṭum. appēṟu muṉ kiṭippiṉum piṉkiṭippiṉum muṭivu oṉṟē. camutāyam iruvakip pēṟukaḷiyum varavēṟkavē ceykiṉṟatu. iḷam peṟṟōrkkum mutiyapeṟṟōrkkum makiḻnili vēṟāyiṉum, camutāyattiṉ maṉanili īriṭattum oṉṟē. atupōla kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta maṟivuk kaṟpiṉiyum, kaḷaviṉ vaḻivārā marapuk kaṟpiṉiyum camutāyam oṉṟupōl nōkkukiṉṟatu; ēṟṟattāḻvu cuṭṭuvatilli.
ilakkiyattil āḷappaṭṭa poruṭkūṟukaḷi oppu nōkkit tiṟaṅkāṇiṉ, ōruṇmi pulaṉākum. ilakkiyappāṭu poruḷkaḷ aṟañcāṉṟaṉavāka vēṇṭum eṉpatilli. nalvāḻvukkuvēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ kavitinayañ cāṉṟaṉavāka vēṇṭum eṉpatum illi. peṟṟōr pēcimuṭikkum tirumaṇap puṇarcciyil, pulavariṉ kaṟpaṉikku iṭamētu? kātalartam vēṭkik koḷḷikku iṭamētu? eṇṇat tuṭippukku iṭamētu? ātaliṉ caṅkaccāṉṟōrkaḷ āṟṟoḻukkup pōṉṟa iyalpumaṇattip pāṭa muṉvara villi. pāṭāmiyiṉāl ataṉ camutāya matippuk kuṉṟa villi.  
marapuneṟic celvākku
maṇa vāḻvukku mēṟcolliya iraṇṭum takka neṟikaḷē eṉṟu tamiḻc camutāyam uṭaṉpaṭṭatu. kaḷavoḻukkamiṉṟiyē taṉik kaṟpumuṟi muṉpu vaḻaṅkiya tilli eṉṟu tolkāppiyap poruḷatikāra ārāycci eṉṉum nūlāciriyar moḻikuvar.19 ik karuttukku muṉṉar yāṉ eṭuttu maṟutta iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ nūṟpā uṇṭaṉṟē. ataṉi ētuk kāṭṭuvar. kaḷavu neṟiyallatu kaṟpukku vēṟu neṟi tamiḻakattu illiyā? ellāt tirumaṇaṅkaṭku muṉṉum kātalarkaḷiṭik kaḷavu nikaḻntatu kol? iṉṉavar karuttu uṇmiyāyiṉ, makaḷ taṉ kaḷavip peṟṟōrkku ēṉ maṟittal vēṇṭum? kaḷavaṟinta peṟṟōr kuṭimikku iḻukkeṉa vekuḷvāṉēṉ? piṉṉar uṭaṉ pōtal eṟṟukkō? kaṟpukkuk kaḷavu iṉṟiyamiyā iyalpu neṟiyāyiṉ, kaḷavukku maṟiyeṉṟa peyar poruntumō? kaḷavut tērcci camutāyam uṭaṉpāṭaḷitta neṟiyāyiṉum, kaḷavāḷarkaḷ tam oḻukkam pulaṉākiviṭumō eṉṟu camutāyattiṟkum kuṭumpattiṟkum añciṉārkaḷ. veṟiyāṭṭu, aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉavarum ovvoru kaṭṭattilum ivvaccavuṇarcciyi aṟikiṉṟōm. maṟi veḷiyātaṟku muṉṉarē, maṟiyeṉa oṉṟu nikaḻntatu eṉṟu teriyāta paṭiyē, maṇam naṭantuviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi talivaṉi varivu muṭukkukiṉṟāḷ; arumpāṭu paṭukiṉṟāḷ.
kaḻiyak kātala rāyiṉum cāṉṟōr
paḻiyoṭu varūum iṉpam veḵkār
variyiṉ evaṉō vāṉtōy veṟpa
kaṇakkali ikukkum kaṟiyivar cilampiṉ
maṇapparuṅ kāmam puṇarntami yaṟiyār
toṉṟiyal marapiṉ maṉṟam ayara 	(akam. 112)
toṉṟiyal marapiṉ maṉṟal eṉa iyalpu maṇatti - peṟṟōr kūṭṭuvikkum maṇattit tōḻi cuṭṭutali niṉika. miḷakuk koṭipaṭarnta maliccāralil nikaḻttiya kāmakkaḷaviṉit taliviyiṉ peṟṟōr aṟiyāta niliyil vittē, marapumaṉṟal ceytuviṭavēṇṭum eṉṟu tōḻi ācippaṭukiṉṟāḷ. kātalarkkuḷ evvaḷavu aṉpiruntālum, nīṭitta kaḷaviṉpam paḻitaruvatu eṉṟu talivaṉi iṭitturikkiṉṟāḷ. kaḷavukku añcukiṉṟaṉar eṉpatu tāṉē ivaṟṟiṉ uṭkiṭi. tamiḻccamutāyattuk kaḷavu neṟi oṉṟallatu vēṟiṉṟeṉiṉ, iv vaccam poruḷaṟṟatu kāṇ. ōrūrkku vaḻi iraṇṭu mūṉṟu iruppiṉ, ivar evvaḻi vantār eṉa ārāytaṟkum viṉātaṟkum poruḷuṇṭu. vaḻiyoṉṟē yāyiṉ, vantavaḻi collāmalē uṇarappaṭum; ottuk koḷḷavum paṭum. iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ nūṟpippatu pōla, kaḷavuneṟiyē neṟiyāyiṉ, makaḷ kaḷavip peṟṟōr uṇartalum uṭaṉpaṭutalumallatu kotitteḻutal poru ḷaṟṟatu. kaṟpukku vaḻi iraṇṭu uṇṭāmātaliṉ, marapuneṟip paṭṭa peṟṟōr kotitteḻuntaṉar eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.
taliviyarum tōḻimārum kūṟum kaḷavup pāṭalkaḷit tokuttu ārāyvōmāyiṉ, uṟṟārkkum ūrārkkum kaḷavi aṟavē maṟittu, virivil tirumaṇam koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉa appeṇkaḷukku irunta nāṇamum kavaliyum nāṭṭamum pulappaṭum. immaṟivu muyaṟciyip paṭāmi varital eṉṟu (1085) tolkāppiyam nūṟpikkum. marapu maṇattit tamariṟ peṟutal (tol. 1444) eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar. kaḷavi muṟṟum maṟittō aṉṟic cevvitāka veḷippaṭuttiyō, eppaṭiyum peṟṟōriṉ iciviyum vāḻttiyum peṟṟu, marapu maṇam koḷḷavēṇṭum eṉṟa peruviḻivu akattiṇip peṇṇāṭkaḷukku iruntatu.
naṉṟā kiṉṟāl tōḻinam vaḷḷiyuḷ
oṉṟinām pāṭa maṟiniṉṟu kēṭṭaruḷi
meṉṟōṭ kiḻavaṉum vantaṉaṉ nuntiyum
maṉṟal vēṅkik kīḻiruntu
maṇanayan taṉaṉam malikiḻa vōṟkē 	(kali. 41)
itu kaḷavu veḷippaṭā muṉṉarē, peṟṟōr mūlam naṭattikkoṇṭa tirumaṇamākum. tōḻiyum taliviyum pāṭiya vaḷḷippāṭṭu oḷintu niṉṟa talivaṉ neñcit tākkiyatu. iṉi, kaḷavu nīḷātu varitalē takum eṉṟu maṇampēca vantāṉ. vantavaṉukkut taṉ makaḷōṭu uṟavuṇṭu eṉṟu tanti aṟiyāṉ. aṟiyāta niliyilē, talivaṉatu viruppattiṟku icintāṉ. etirpārā in nallicivu, kāval niliyattu eḻuti viyā muṉṉarē iḻanta poruḷ kiṭittāṟ pōṉṟa makiḻcciyit tōḻikku aḷittatu. tōṭ kiḻavaṉum vantaṉaṉ; nuntiyum nayantaṉaṉ eṉṟa aṇukiya coṉṉaṭiyāl, kaḷavu pulappaṭavilli eṉpatu aṟiyalām. im maṇamuṭipu paṭāmi varital eṉpataṟku ilakkiyamām.
entiyum yāyum uṇarak kāṭṭi
oḷitta ceyti veḷippaṭak kiḷantapiṉ
malikeḻu veṟpaṉ talivan tirappa
naṉṟupuri koḷkiyiṉ oṉṟā kiṉṟē     	(kuṟun. 374)
itu peṟṟōrukkuk kaḷavi veḷippaṭutti muṭittuk koṇṭa tirumaṇamām. kaḷavoḻukkatti oḷitta ceyti eṉṟum, talivaṉ kēṭka varivukkup peṟṟōr uṭaṉ paṭṭati naṉṟu puri koḷki eṉṟum palkāyaṉār pāṭuvar. iṉi, nērvaḻiyallatu kuṟippuvaḻi payaṉpaṭātu eṉṟa niliyil, kātaṟ kaṉṉiyar tuṇivu talikkoṇṭu, veḷippaṭu collāl kaḷavip peṟṟōr muṉṉum aṟivippar eṉavum, uṟutiyum uṇmiyum terinta aṟivuṭip peṟṟōr kaṟpukku āvaṉavē ceyvar eṉavum, ivvāṟṟāl tamiḻc camutāyam muṭivil kaṟpukkuc cevicāyttut talivaṇaṅkiṟṟu eṉavum, ik kuṟuntokip pāṭṭāl cevvitiṉ viḷaṅkum.
kaṟpukkup piṉṉiṭal yāṇṭum tamiḻoḻukkam aṉṟu. ituvē akattiṇi kaṟpikkum peruntamiḻmi eṉa aṟika. uṭaṉpōya makaḷ kaḷavuk kātalaṉōṭu ūrvantu taṉ vīṭṭil vaḻakkampōl maṇañceytu koḷḷa vēṇṭum eṉṟa marapuppaṟṟu peṇ vīṭṭārkku iruntatu. emmaṉi vatuvi naṉmaṇam kaḻika (aiṅ. 344), āṉātu tuyarum em kaṇ iṉitu paṭīiyar emmaṉi muntuṟat tarumō (akam. 195) eṉṟu, iṅṅaṉ uṭaṉpōkiyāḷiṉ aṉṉi pērācip paṭukiṉṟāḷ.
marapu nāṭṭam ellārkkum ūṟikkiṭantami mēlic cāṉṟukaḷāl teḷiyalām. kaḷavu neṟippaṭṭa varivu eṉpataṉik kuṟuntokip pāṭṭum (374), kaḷavu neṟiyāka viṭātu marapu neṟiyākkiya varivu eṉpataṉik kalit tokip pāṭṭum (41) kāṭṭi niṟkiṉṟaṉa. palvēṟu tuṟi kaḷiyum attuṟit tōṟṟattiṟku uriya maṉappāṅku kaḷiyum nōkkiṉ, tamiḻc camutāyattil marapu neṟikkirunta naṉmatippu veḷiyākum. akattiṇip pāṭalkaḷiliruntu aḵtāvatu peyar cuṭṭappaṭāt taṉmip pāṭalkaḷiliruntu ituvari mēṟkōḷkaḷ kaṇṭōm. puṟappāṭalkaḷum, aḵtāvatu peyar cuṭṭum varalāṟṟup pāṭalkaḷum ikkarutti valiyuṟuttak kāṇpōm. caṅkac cāṉṟōr kapilar cāṉṟōr palarāl matikkap peṟṟavar; muḻukka muḻukkak kaḷaviyal pāṭiya kātaṟ pulavar; vaḷḷal pāri ivartam naṇpaṉ, pāri iṟantapiṉ, avaṉ peṇ makkaḷ iruvarkkum takka kaṇavarit tēṭum poṟuppu kapilarukku ēṟpaṭṭatu. kaḷavu pāṭiya kapilar eṉ ceytār? irumakaḷiyum aḻittuk koṇṭu kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ viccikkōṉiṭam ceṉṟār; parantōṅku ciṟappiṉi yuṭiya pāri makaḷiri yāṉ koṭuppa maṇantu koḷ eṉṟu vēṇṭiṉār. viccikkōṉ maṇakka uṭaṉpaṭavilli. ataṉpiṉ kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ iruṅkōvēḷ pāl ceṉṟār; in naṅkiyari yāṉ koṭuppa maṇañceytu      koḷka eṉṟu irantār. iruṅkō vēḷum icintā ṉilli. ivvaralāṟṟuk kuṟippukkaḷip puṟanāṉūṟṟil aṟikiṉṟōm.
parantōṅku ciṟappiṟ pāri makaḷir
yāṉē, paricilaṉ maṉṉum antaṇaṉ; nīyē
variciyil vaṇakkum vāṇmēm paṭunaṉ
niṉakku yāṉ koṭuppak koṇmati	(puṟam. 200)
ivaryār eṉkuvi yāyiṉ ivarē...
neṭumāp pāri makaḷir; yāṉē
tanti tōḻaṉ ivareṉ makaḷir
antaṇaṉ pulavaṉ koṇṭuvan taṉaṉē...
yāṉtara ivarik koṇmati	(puṟam. 201)
ip puṟanāṉūṟṟaṭikaḷ marapuneṟi vaḻakkic collukiṉṟaṉa. tammuḷē puṇarnta tāmaṟi puṇarcciyar (kali. 9) eṉpatu kaḷavuneṟiyiṉ ilakkaṇam. īṇṭō, niṉakku yāṉ koṭuppak koḷ, yāṉ tara ivarik koḷ eṉṟu kapilar taṟcuṭṭic collukiṉṟār. itu marapu maṇappēccākum. ip puṟanāṉūṟṟaṭikaḷik koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppak koḷvatuvē (tol. 1087) eṉṉum akavilakkaṇattōṭu oppiṭṭuk koḷka. iv varalāṟṟu meycceyti kaṟpippatu eṉṉa? tamiḻar tirumaṇattukkut tāmē tērum kaḷavoṉṟu tāṉ neṟiyāka iruntatu eṉpatilli. tamar koṭuppak koḷḷal eṉṉum maṟṟoṉṟum neṟiyāka iruntatu eṉṟu teḷikiṉṟōm. inneṟiyum paḻimiyuṭiyatu, paravik kiṭantatu, marapeṉa matikkappaṭṭatu eṉṟa karuttellām toṉṟuiyal marapiṉ maṉṟal (akam. 112) eṉṟa toṭarāl valiyuṟum. marapu neṟikku matippiṉṟēl pāriyiṉ perumi ciṟumippaṭak kapilar anneṟiyi mēṟkoḷḷāraṉṟō!

V
marapuvaḻik kātalar
kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta paḻiya kātalarkkum, kaḷaviṉ vaḻi vārāp putiya kātalarkkum, iṉpaniliyilum aṟaniliyilum vēṟupāṭuṇṭō? maṉanilikaḷ vēṟupaṭap pulavarkaḷ pāṭiyatuṇṭō? eṉṟu kāṇaṟkuc caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷit turuviṉ vēṟupāṭu yātum kaṇṭilōm. kūṭṭukkaḷippil irutiṟattārum tammi maṟakkiṉṟaṉar. uriyāṭalil yārum iḷittārilar. ūṭal muṭivil uyiroṉṟākiṉṟaṉar.
tamarnamak kītta talināḷ iraviṉ
uvarnīṅku kaṟpiṉem uyiruṭam paṭuvi
muruṅkāk kaliṅkam muḻuvatum vaḷiip
perumpuḻuk kuṟṟaniṉ piṟinutaṟ poṟiviyar
uṟuvaḷi yāṟṟac ciṟuvari tiṟaveṉa
ārva neñcamoṭu pōrvi vavvaliṉ
uṟikaḻi vāḷiṉ uruvupeyarn timippa
maṟitiṟaṉ aṟiyā ḷākip piyeṉa
nāṇiṉaḷ iṟiñci yōḷē... ...
irumpal kūntal iruḷmaṟi oḷittē      	(akam. 136)
eṉ uyirpaṭu meyyāḷē, putiya āṭiyāl muḻutum pōrtta niṉuṭal puḻukkamaṭiyātā? kāṟṟu paṇicey taṟkēṉum iṭaṅkoṭu eṉṟu colli, ārva neñcattāl āṭiyip puṟappaṭuttiṉēṉ. uṟiyik kaḻitta vāḷpōl avaḷ mēṉi oḷiṟiṟṟu. kūntalāṭiyāl aṟṟam maṟittu nāṇi ocintāḷ eṉṟu marapuneṟik kātalaṉ talināḷ iraviṉ pāyal viḷiyāṭṭip pala nāṭceṉṟu niṉik kiṉṟāṉ. kaḷavukkātal uṟavillātāriṭattum cellum, uṟavuṭi yāriṭattum cellum. iv viruvaki nili marapuk kātalukkum ēṟkum. peṟṟōrkaḷ tammakkaṭku uṟavukaṭantum ūrkaṭantum maṇaṅkāṇa muyalvar. tāyvaḻiyālō tanti vaḻiyālō toṭarpillā eṭṭākki yiṭattum maṇampēci muṭippar. yātum ūrē yāvarum kēḷir eṉṟa niliyi ik koḷvaṉi koṭuppaṉikaḷiṟ pārkkiṉṟōm. ittakiya oru maṇavarivip pulavar cempulap peyal nīrār arumi yākap pāṭukiṉṟār.
yāyum ñāyum yārā kiyarō
entiyum nuntiyum emmuṟik kēḷir
yāṉum nīyum evvaḻi aṟitum
cempulap peyalnīr pōla
aṉpuṭi neñcan tāṅkalan taṉavē	(kuṟun. 40)
itu peṟṟōr muṭittiṭṭa tirumaṇak kātalaṉ puṇarcciyil makiḻntu kūṟum kaṭṭuriyākum. nammuḷ muṉṉuṟavilli. nam takapparkkum muṉ aṟimukam illi. nam tāyarum terintavarkaḷ illi, yātum uṟavillā nām, maṇṇaka nilaṉum vāṉaka nīrum kalantu oṉṟāṉāṟ pōla, aṉpiṉāl uḷḷaṅ kalantuviṭṭōm eṉṟu uṭal mayaṅkiya kaṇavaṉ putunaṭpiṉ āḻatti aṟivuṟuttukiṉṟāṉ. ippāṭalukkuk kuṟuntokiyil vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷa tuṟi vēṟu. iyaṟkip puṇarcci eytiya talivaṉ namakkuḷ pirivu ētu eṉṟu collit talimakaḷi āṟutal ceytāṉ eṉpatu attuṟi. ikkaḷavut tuṟiyum poruntumeṉiṉum, kuravar kūṭṭiya kaṟpiṉ mutaṟ puṇarcciyākak koḷḷutal iṉṉum cālum eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. iruvakik kuravariyum niṉikiṉṟāṉ, niṉippikkiṉṟāṉ. ātaliṉ, kuravariṟ puṇarcciyākak koḷka. kuṟiñcit tiṇik kaḷavuttuṟip pāṭalil mutaṟporuḷ karupporuḷ peritum iṭam peṟutal uṇṭu. puṇarcci kūṟum ik kuṟuntokip pāṭalil mutalum karuvum iṭam peṟṟila; maliyō piṟavō kūṟappeṟṟila. ātaliṉ, illattu nikaḻnta kaṟpup puṇarcciyākavē koḷka.
kaḷaviṉ vaḻivārāk kātalartam iṉpanili akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭṭālum (136), aṉpunili kuṟuntokip pāṭṭālum (40) teḷiyavarum. enniliyilum ivarkkum kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta kātalarkkum akattiṇi vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭavilli. aṉpuṭip peṟṟōr tammakkaḷ nalvāḻvu vāḻavēṇṭum eṉṟē eṇṇuvar. aṟivum uṭiya peṟṟōr aṉṉavartam nōkkattik kuṟippāl aṟintē maṇamuṭivu ceykuvar. ātaliṉ peṟṟōr muṭipellām piḷḷikaḷiṉ kātal nāṭṭattiṟku māṟāka irukkum eṉṟu collutaṟ killi. ottiruppaṉavē perumpālaṉa. kaṟpuvaḻippaṭṭa kaḷavukaḷē akattiṇiyil iṭampeṟukiṉṟaṉa. atupōl attiṇiyil piḷḷikaḷ ottukkoḷḷum peṟṟōr maṇamuṭipukaḷē iṭam peṟukiṉṟaṉa. ātaliṉ pulavarkaḷ vēṟupaṭap pāṭutaṟku vitiyilli. kaḷavuneṟiyākuka - marapu neṟiyākuka. enneṟik kaṇ kātalarkaḷiṭi uḷḷappuṇarcci - orumi neñcam - uṇṭu. anneṟiyellām aintiṇip pāṟpaṭum eṉṟu aṟivōmāka. evvaḻit tirumaṇattarāyiṉum, uḷḷam oṉṟiya kātal māntar avvorumikeṭa yātum ceyyār. ivvorumi keṭātavāṟu yātuñ ceyvar. kātalukku orumi ilakkaṇam kūṟum ittamiḻiyam maṉita viṉattiṟkē potumi.  
uṇmik kātalar uṟavuk kiṭiyē
tiṇmi kulikkum tīmikaḷ tōṉṟā
ētuk kaṇṭāl māṟik koḷḷum
kātal tāṉum kāta lāmō
piḷavupaṭutaṟkuriya kāraṇam iruppiṉum, piriyā orumi vāḻvē kātal eṉṟu ulakappulavar cekappiriyar illaṟattiṉ āḻatti aḷantu kāṭṭuvar.20
piṟcaṅka kālatteḻunta tamiḻ mutaṟkāppiyamām cilappatikāram kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyar tirumaṇatti orukāti muḻutum virittup pāṭukiṉṟatu. yātoru kaḷavuccuvaṭum āṇṭu illi. itu kuravar muṭitta maṇam eṉṟu iḷaṅkō veḷippaṭiyākak kūṟuvar.
avari,
iruperuṅ kuravarum oruperu nāḷāl
maṇavaṇi kāṇa makiḻntaṉar makiḻntuḻi
yāṉi yeruttat taṇiyiḻiyār mēlirīi
mānakark kīntār maṇam
kuravar muṭivik kuḻantikaḷ maṟukkavilli. maṇam maṇamāka naṭantatu. ārāk kātalil iruvarum kiyaṟṟaṉar eṉṟu maṉi yaṟampaṭutta kāti muḻutum paṭikkiṉṟōm. pāmpiṉ puṇarcci pōlat toliyāta iṉpamelām tuṉṉiṉār eṉpatu veṇpā. kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyartam uṭampaṭu puṇarcci kūṟumukattāl. uḷḷam puṇarntirunta niliyi iḷaṅkō kāṇacceyvar. ikkātik kaṇṇum kaḷavuk kuṟippilli eṉpatum niṉiyattakum. kātalāyiṉ kaḷavu ceyyum eṉṟum, kaḷavoḻukkattārē kātalar eṉac collutaṟku uriyār eṉṟum karutalāmō eṉiṉ, atu piḻi. kātal eṉpatu uḷḷappuṇarcci; uḷḷap puṇarikaḷellām kātalarkaḷē. ātaliṉ, kātalaṟ piriyāmal kavavukki ñekiḻāmal (1,91) kayamalark kaṇṇiyum kātaṟ koḻunaṉum(2, 11) tīrāk kātaliṉ tirumukam nōkki (2, 36) kātali taṉṉoṭu katir celvataṉmuṉ (15, 111) kātalaṉ taṉ vīvum kātali nī paṭṭatūum (29, 7) eṉṟāṅku cilappatikārattil kōvalaṉum kaṇṇakiyum kātaṟ coṟkaḷāl pāṭappaṭṭaṉar. kaṟpukkuk kāppiyam aruḷiya kaṇṇaki nallāḷi viṭak kāṭṭāvāḷ yārkol? avaḷ maṇam marapu neṟippaṭṭatu. ātaliṉ anneṟikkuk kuṟi eṉṉa colla muṭiyum? ārvap piṇippu (kaḷaviyal) aṟavē illāviṭattum piṟaneṟiyāṉ vanta maṇaṅkaḷ nilippuṭiyaṉavāka uḷa eṉpar keṉṉattu vākkar.21 kaḷaviṉvaḻi nikaḻātēyum uṇṭu ulakak kaṟpu; aḵtu ittuṇic ciṟappiṉṟu eṉa eḻutukiṟār iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uriyāciriyar (pak. 124), ulakak kaṟpu eṉṟu ivar kuṟippatu marapuvaḻi maṇatti. ilakkiyap paṭippiṟkut tuṟipala uṭiya kaḷavu neṟiyil vāyppiruppatu pōla marapuneṟiyil illi, ātaliṉ ulakak kaṟpuc ciṟavātu eṉpatu uriyāciriyar karuttāyiṉ, (avar aṅṅaṉam karutavilli) aḵtu okkumaṉ, ilakkiyam nōkkik kūṟātu vāḻkkic ciṟappunōkkik kūṟiṉārāyiṉ, kaṟpukkaṭam pūṇṭa teyvak kaṇṇakiyiṉ maṇamē taṉ maṟuppākum. mēlum akat tiṇiyil ulakak kaṟpiṟku iṭamilli eṉṟu ivar karutuvatu, ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam (tol. 998) eṉṉum akakkaruttiṟkup perumuraṇām. akattiṇi ākāyattiṟ pūtta kaṟpaṉi yilakkiyamaṉṟu; nalla tamiḻc camutāyam tanta vaḻakkilakkiyam eṉṟu aṟiyavēṇṭum. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷ uriyāciriyarum, avvāciriyar aṉiya piṟarum nam camutāya vaḻakki maṟantum tuṟantum eḻutum pōtellām piḻi eḻak kāṇkiṉṟōm. 
kaḷavuneṟi oṉṟutāṉ tirumaṇattukku neṟiyāka iruntatu eṉṟu tuṇitaṟkō, anneṟitāṉ ciṟappiṭam peṟṟiruntatu eṉṟu tuṇitaṟkō ilakkiyattum cāṉṟilli. tamiḻc camutāya vaḻakkam cāṉṟāmāṟilli. kuravar tāmē muṭikkum marapuneṟi eṉa oṉṟu iruntatu. iyalpākac camutāyattup paravikkiṭantatu. ilakkiyattum kaṟputtiṇiyil kaḷavupōl pāṭappeṟṟatu eṉṟa nilikaḷi itukāṟum kaṇṭōm. maṇanili oru camutāyatti uruvākkum peru niliyākum. putiya camutāya iṉaṅkaḷil maṇaneṟikaḷ oḻuṅkupaṭuvatilli. av viṉattārkkum itutāṉ maṇamuṟiyeṉa ēṟka maṉam pakkuvappaṭuvatilli. tamiḻc camutāyam pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉē payiṉṟu parantu ōṅkit tamiḻmoḻi pōl oḻuṅkupaṭṭuviṭṭatu. maṇa muṟikaḷ neṟippaṭṭaṉa. makkaḷ maṉaṅkaḷ vaḻippaṭṭaṉa. ātaliṉ koṭuppak koḷḷutal eṉṉum muṟi ellār neñcattum iyalpākap patintu kiṭantatu. makkaṭku maṇañceytu vippatip peṟṟōrkaḷ tam illaṟak kaṭamiyākak koṇṭaṉar. palar añcuvatupōla, vaḷarccikkum māṟutalukkum marapu iṭiyūṟākātu. pōli māṟutalkaḷiyē marapu taṭukkum; avaṟṟit taṭukkavallatum ākum. marapum aṟivum iṇintu cellum tamiḻc camutāyattil, iḷiyavarkaḷ tāmē tērntukoḷḷum kaḷavu neṟiyum pōṟṟappaṭṭatu, ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl parappappaṭṭatu, cāṉṟōrkaḷāl pāṭappeṟṟatu. ilakkaṇam vakukkap peṟṟatu. marapu neṟikaḷip paṉmāṇum viḷakki variyum ivvamayattuk kaḷavu neṟiyiyum uṭaṉniṉintu koḷka. koṇṭāṟṟāṉ tamiḻc camutāyattiṉ nākarika muḻumiyum, akavilakkiyattiṉ muḻuvaṉappum aṟiyamuṭiyum eṉṟu valiyuṟuttuval.

caṅkap piṉṉūlkaḷ
piṟkālat tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ marapuneṟiyiṉum kaḷavu neṟikku ōr ēṟṟamum mutaṉmiyum koṭuttuviṭṭaṉar. kaḷavu neṟitāṉ kaṟpukku muṉ nikaḻum tamiḻneṟi eṉṟa eṇṇatti vitittuviṭṭaṉar. tiruttakkatēvar cīvakaṉ pala maṇattip puṉiyum pōtellām kaḷaviṉiyum kāṭci aiyam mutalāṉa tuṟikaḷi vittup pāṭat tavaṟuvatilli. maṇattiṟkumuṉ kātalartam kaṇkaḷ kavvikkoṇṭaṉa eṉṟu kaṟpikkum ōr ilakkiyap pērāci tēvarukku iruppak kāṇkiṉṟōm. tēvar vaḻi vanta kamparum mitilikkāṭcip paṭalattil irāmaṉum cītiyum uḷḷam oṉṟiya kaḷavuppuṇarcciyic cuviyūṟa amittuk kāṭṭuvar, aṇṇalum nōkkiṉāṉ avaḷum nōkkiṉāḷ, iruvarum māṟippukku itayam eytiṉār eṉṟu kaṇvaḻik kātalip puṉivar. ivvamippu vāṉmīkattil illi; irāma kātiyit tamiḻiṟ pāṭiya kampar tamiḻ neṟikkēṟpa vakutturittār eṉṟu kaṟṟār palar eṭutturikkiṉṟaṉar. muṟkūṟiyāṅkuk kaḷavum tamiḻiyamē yātalāl, tamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷil varivukku muṉ kātalarkaḷiṉ kāṭciyum tuṇivum kavaliyum uḷḷam puṇaralum, meyyuṟu puṇarcciyumkūṭa iṭampeṟal tavaṟaṉṟu; naṉmuṟiyākum. ātaliṉ tēvarum kamparum neñcak kalappuk kūṟiyapiṉ tirumaṇam ceyvippati varavēṟpōm. iruvarum aṅṅaṉam kūṟātu, tam kāppiya māntarkaṭkut tirumaṇam ceytu vittiruntālum ammuṟiyiyum varavēṟpōm, atuvum tamiḻiyamātaliṉ.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ marapuneṟippaṭi kāppiyam amittavar. avar pāṭiya māntarkaḷ tamiḻnilattup piṟantu tamiḻpayiṉṟa tamiḻ makkaḷ. eṉiṉum kaḷavuk kāṭciyi āciriyar cuṭṭavilli eṉpati uṉṉuka. cilappatikāram uṇmiyāka naṭanta varalāṟu kūṟuvatu. kaṇṇaki kōvalaṉār tirumaṇam marapuppaṭi naṭantirukkumēl, ataṉi māṟṟip puṉintu eḻutuvatu eḻuttaṟamaṉṟu eṉa oruvar etiriṭalām. varalāṟṟuk kāppiyamāyiṉum, kātaṟ kaḷaṅkaḷik kaṟpaṉi vaṇṇap paṭutti iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭuk kāṭṭuvataṟkup pulavaṉukku ilakkiyavurimi uṇṭu. tīrāk kātalāl kaṇṇakiyiṉ tirumukatti nōkkik kōvalaṉ iṉpattil puriyāk kaṭṭuri kaḷip poḻintu nalam pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ; ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ vaṭanāṭu ceṉṟa pirivukkāli, vañcinakar araṇmaṉiyil vatiyum avaṉ kōpperuntēvi nāṉilap pāṇiyum kēṭṭuc ceviliyar āṟṟavum āṟṟātirukkiṉṟāḷ. ivvakik karuttukkaḷālum pala varikaḷālum ilakkiya urimiyuṇar vuṭiyavar iḷaṅkō eṉṟu aṟiyalām. kaḷavu neṟiyākap pāṭum peṟṟiyum tuṇivum avar illātārilar. pāṭi yiruppiṉ, ataṉi nām varavēṟṟup pōṟṟātiruppōmō? mutaṟkāppiyam paṭitta iḷaṅkō aṭikaḷiṉ cuvaṭṭiṉar tēvarum kamparum eṉṟu vaḻimaruṅku cuṭṭā tiruppōmō?
itukāṟum āynta viḷakkattiṉ nōkkam eṉṉa? tamiḻk kāppiyaṅkaḷ maṟkoṇṭa marapuneṟi kaḷavuneṟi yiraṇṭum camutāyattiṟkum akattiṇikkum poruntiya tamiḻ neṟikaḷē eṉavum, kaḷavuneṟik kātal pāṭiṉāṟṟāṉ tamiḻ nākarikamākum eṉakkoḷvatu orupāṟ piḻiyuṭittu eṉavum teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkum. cuntararum kārikkālammi yārum innilattut tōṉṟiya nammavar; varalāṟṟu māntarkaḷ, cuntarar paraviyār tirumaṇattik kaḷavu neṟiyākavum (naṟperum pāṉmi kūṭṭa naki potintu ilaṅku cevvāy viṟpuri nutaliṉ vēṟkaṇ viḷaṅkiḻiyavarik kaṇṭār (periya. 285), ammiyār tirumaṇatti marapu neṟiyākavum, munti marapiṉukku ēṟkum muṟimi maṇam (periya. 1729) cēkkiḻār tamiḻc camutāyattiṉ muḻukkūṟum tōṉṟat tam kāppiyak kaliyip paṭittuḷḷār, ip piṟkuṟippum mēli ārāyccikkut tuṇiceytal kāṇka.

VI
kaḷavuvaḻik kātalar         
kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta illaṟattār vāḻkki iṉimi mikkatu. avvaḻik kaṇavaṉ parattiyi nāṭāṉ, parattiyiṟ pirivu kāraṇamāka maṉivi ūṭāḷ eṉṟu kaḷavuneṟikku ēṟṟam kūṟalāmā? paratti nāṭṭam kaḷaviṉ vaḻi vārāk kātalaṉiṭattē kāṇappaṭum; kōvalaṉ ataṟku oru cāṉṟu eṉṟu marapuneṟikkut tāḻvu kūṟalāmā? eṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamum kūṟutaṟkilli. kaḷavu neṟiyilum paratti nāṭikaḷ palar kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. paratti nāṭāk kaṟput tiṇmiyik kaḷavuneṟik kātalaṉmārum uṭiyarāka illi. iruvakik kātalarkkum paratti yoḻukkattil vēṟupāṭiṉṟēṉum, avvoḻukkatti iṭittuk kāṭṭum muṟiyil vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
maliyiṭi yiṭṭa nāṭṭarum allar;
marantali tōṉṟā ūrarum allar; 
kaṇṇiṟ kāṇa naṇṇuvaḻi yiruntum,
kaṭavuḷ naṇṇiya pālōr pōla
orīi yoḻukum eṉṉikkup
pariyaleṉ maṉyāṉ paṇṭoru kālē	(kuṟun. 203)
vēṟṟunāṭṭu vēṟṟu ūrarākiya nam talivar kaḷavuk kālattu malipārātu, maracceṟivu pārātu, cēymi pārātu, nammip pārkka orunāḷum tavaṟātu vantār. aṉṟu nammāṭṭu avarkku irunta ārvam atu. tirumaṇak kaṟpākiyapiṉ avar ārvam ceṉṟa tici vēṟu. kaṭavuḷi aṭinta tuṟavikaḷ illaṟatti nīttu oḻukuvāraṉṟē; parattik kaṭavuḷip (kali. 93) paṟṟiya eṉ kaṇavarum aṅṅaṉam ākiviṭṭār. ippōtu ōr ūril oru teruvil nam kaṇ paṭātavāṟu tuṟavi naṭatti varukiṉṟār. ataṉik keṭuppatu aḻakaṉṟu. avar ārvam kāṭṭiya paṇṭik kaḷavuk kālattu nāṉum aṉpu kāṭṭiṉēṉ. iṉṟu aṉpu māṟiya avarmāṭṭu namakku aṉpu eṅṅaṉē tōṉṟum? ivvāṟu talivi parattat talivaṉukkuc cārpāka pēciya tōḻiyi maṟutturikkiṉṟāḷ; avaṉatu aṉpu poyyatu eṉṟu muntiya kaḷavic cuṭṭikkāṭṭik kaḻaṟukiṉṟāḷ. it talivaṉ illattiṟ pallāṇṭu nallavaṉāka oḻukip piṉṉar parattamikku māṟiṉāṉ eṉpatu, paṇṭoru kāl eṉṟa kālac cēymik kuṟippāl aṟiyalām. kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta vēṟucilar maṇanta putumi yilēyē maṇamaṉi (parattivīṭu) nāṭiṉar eṉṟu aṟivikkum pāṭalkaḷum uḷa;
nalla colli maṇantiṉi
nīyēṉ eṉṟa tevaṉkol aṉṉāy    	(aiṅ. 22)
tēṟṟañ ceytunap puṇarntiṉit
tākkaṇaṅ kāva tevaṉkol aṉṉāy 	(aiṅ. 23)
kātaṟ kiḷavikaḷ pala colli maṇantāṉ; orukālum tavaṟākap piriyēṉ eṉṟāṉ; cūḷuṟavu tantu nampumpaṭi ceytu nammip puṇarntāṉ. iṉṟu eṉṉāṉāṉ? nām vaṇaṅkum teyvam iṉṟu nammi varuttum teyvamāyiṟṟu eṉat talivi kaḷaviṟ colvēṟu kaṟpiṟ ceyal vēṟu eṉṟu oḻukkap puṟattāṉi iṭitturikkiṉṟāḷ. nalla colli maṇantāṉ, tēṟṟañ ceytu puṇarntāṉ eṉṟu talivikku niṉivōṭuvatāl, ivaḷ maṇam aṇimiyil nikaḻntatu eṉavum, maṇanta aṇimiyilēyē kaḷavukkātalaṉ parattik kāmukaṉ āyiṉāṉ eṉavum koḷḷalām. kaḷavuk kātalarkaḷum parattiyāṭak kāṇutaliṉ, marapuneṟiyiṉum kaḷavuneṟikku eṉṉa ēṟṟam colla muṭiyum? meyyāka nōkkiṉ, ennilik kaṇṇum taṉ taliviyallātāḷi eṇṇā oḻukkattiṭpam kaḷavuneṟiyāl uṇṭāka vēṇṭum.    
kaḷavuneṟiyātu vāḻnāḷkāṟum ōruyir taṉkātal cellutaṟkuriya maṟupāluyirit tāṉē tērntu koḷḷum iyaṟki neṟi. anneṟikkut tāṉum parattimiyit taṭukkum āṟṟal illeṉiṉ, melliya pāluṇarcciyiṉ vaṉmiyiyum tīmiyiyum eṉ colvatu? uṭkuṭintu pukūum nuṇṇiya vērmuṉikaḷāl malikaḷum matilkaḷum piḷakkavilliyā? kaḷavuk kātalukkut taṭupput tiṟam illi eṉṟu keṉṉattu vākkar oru viḷakkam ceykiṉṟār:- viḻippuṇarcci kātaṟ poruḷiṭattu illā nayaṅkaḷi iruppaṉavākak kaṟpikkiṉṟatu. illā aḻakukaḷi vaṇṇap paṭuttik kāṇkiṉṟatu. illā uṇarccikaḷi ēṟṟic cuvik kiṉṟatu. kātalikkappaṭṭa poruḷ eytutaṟku arum poruḷāka irukkumvari, kaṟpitta ēṟṟaṅkaḷukku vāḻvu uṇṭu. viyappiṉ viḷinta poymmikaḷ, maṇamāṉa piṉṉar maṟin toḻiyum22 parattami paṟṟiya marutappāṭalkaḷil viyappiṉ viḷivi aṟikiṉṟōm.
vēmpiṉa piṅkāyeṉ tōḻi tariṉē
tēmpūṅ kaṭṭi eṉṟaṉir; iṉiyē
pāri paṟampiṟ paṉiccuṉit teṇṇīr
tiit tiṅkaḷ taṇṇiya tariṉum
veyya vuvarkkum eṉṟaṉir
aiya aṟṟāl aṉpiṉ pālē	(kuṟun. 196)
nalla vēppaṅkāyi eṉ talivi taruvāḷēl, avaḷ kippaṭṭa akkacappuk kāyit tiṉṟu, tēṉil ūṟiya vellak kaṭṭipōla iṉikkiṉṟatē eṉṟu nīr colliya oru kālam uṇṭu. atutāṉ kaḷavukkālam. ippōtu kāma vēṇavā nīṅkiyapiṉ, kuḷirccikkup peyarpōṉa pāriyiṉ paṟampuccuṉi naṉṉīri avaḷ tantālum, cūṭāy uppuk karikkum eṉṟu vāṅka maṟukkiṉṟīr. num aṉpiṉ iyalpu iruvēṟu paṭṭatu eṉṟu tōḻi oḻukkam puṟampōnta talivaṉukku oppiṭṭuk kāṭṭukiṉṟāḷ. ippāṭalāciriyar miḷik kantaṉār kātaṟṟoṭakkattiṉ kavarcciyiyum poliviyum kālappōkkil ataṉ taṇiviyum ēmāṟṟattiyum nērmāṟupaṭap pulappaṭuttum uḷaviyalpu aṟiviyalukkum naṭiyiyalukkum poruntuvatākum. ēṉatu cuvippiṉu nī kitoṭṭatu vāṉōr amutam puriyumāl emakkeṉa(1091) eṉṟu tolkāppiyar vēṭkiyiṉ maṉavimmali veḷippaṭuttuvar. kātalārvam tavaṟaṉṟu; takuvatu eṉiṉum, ārvamikutiyil cōrvu tōṉṟum. ēṟṟattiṟkēṟpa iṟakkam uṇṭu. maṇanta toṭakkattuk kōvalaṉ kaṇṇakiyip pukaḻum ārva varivukaḷ akarātiyil illi. mācaṟu poṉṉē, valampuri muttē, kācaṟu viriyē, karumpē, tēṉē, maruntē, maṇiyē, amiḻtē, iciyē eṉṟu ulavāk kaṭṭuri pala pārāṭṭukiṉṟāṉ. ivvārvap puṉiviṉ viḷiveṉṉa? cila āṇṭiṟkuḷ kaṇṇakiyi maṟantāṉ; pala āṇṭu mātavipāl mayaṅkik kiṭantāṉ. ārvac coṟkaḷāl kōvalaṉ mātaviyip pukaḻavilli eṉpati niṉika. 

VII
karaṇam (caṭaṅku muṟi)
kaṟpu eṉṟāl koṇṭa koḻunaṉpāl aṉput tiṇmi eṉpatu poruḷeṉa ellārum potuvāka aṟivōm. ipporuḷ poruḷē. akattiṇikkaṇ varum kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉpaṉavō ilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷ. ivi potuvāka nām aṟiyum poruḷōṭu iṉṉum virinta poruḷiyuṭiyaṉa. maṇamum maṇappiṉ nikaḻum kātaṟ ceykikaḷum ellām kaṟpeṉappaṭum (tol. 1444). paṉmāṇ kaṟpiṉ niṉkiḷi mutalōr (163) eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟut toṭaril kaṟpeṉṉum colliṉ poruṭparappi aṟiyalām. peṇṇiṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta oḻukkam eṉṟu pōṟṟukiṉṟōmē kaṟpu eṉṉum tiṇmi. attiṇmi tirumaṇattāl tōṉṟuva tilli. tirumaṇat tiṟkuppiṉ vaḷarvuṟuvatilli, tirumaṇa māyiṟṟu eṉpataṟkākat tēyvatilli. kaḷavilum kaṟpuṇṭu, kaḷaviṟku muṉṉum kaṟpuuṇṭu. kaṟpoṭu piṟakkiṉṟāḷ peṇ. kaḷaviṟku muṉ peṇṇuḷḷam oruvaṉi niṉiyavilli yātaliṉ, kaṟpu veḷippaṭa ētuvilli. kaḷavil oruvaṉi nāṭumpōtē kaṟpoṭu nāṭukiṉṟāḷ. kaṟputtiṇmi avaḷuk kiṉṟēl, talivaṉ varuvaḻi ētattiṟku ēṉ kavalkiṉṟāḷ? ēṉ veṟiyāṭṭi vilakka muṟpaṭukiṉṟāḷ? ēṉ aṟattoṭu niṟkiṉṟāḷ? ellām paliyāta pōtu, piṟanta illattiyum, peṟṟōriyum, aṟinta cūḻniliyiyum oru noṭiyil veṟuttu, uṭaṉpōkat tuṇivatu ēṉ? ivvellām iṉpattiṉ poruṭṭā? illi, illi. oruvaṉi niṉitta uḷḷak kāppiṉ poruṭṭu. ātaliṉ kaṟpu eṉṉum col - oḻukkattiṇmi eṉṉum vaḻakkup poruṇmi uṭiya accol - kaḷavup pāṭalkaḷilum payilak kaṟkiṉṟōm.
añcilam poṭukki añciṉaḷ vantu
tuñcūr yāmattum muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ
āṉṟa kaṟpiṟ cāṉṟa periya
ammā ariviyō allaḷ        	(akam. 168)
iṟanta kaṟpiṉāṭ kevvam paṭaraṉmiṉ
ciṟantāṉi vaḻipaṭīic ceṉṟaṉaḷ  	(kali. 9)
iṭiccura maruṅkiṉ avaḷtamar eytik
kaṭikkoṇṭu peyarttaliṟ kalaṅkañar eytik
kaṟpoṭu puṇarnta keḷavi	(tol. 86)
1. peṟṟōrikkarantu, kāṟcilampiṉ nāvikkaṭṭi, naḷḷeṉ yāmattu vantu ārattaḻuviya taliviyi, āṉṟa kaṟpiṟcāṉṟa perumiyuṭiyavaḷ eṉṟu talivaṉ avaḷ kaṟpiṉ āṟṟali uṇarkiṉṟāṉ. 2. makaḷ uṭaṉpōyiṉāḷ eṉṟu alintu tiriyum cevilikku mukkōṟ pakavarkaḷ, aṉṉa kaṟputtiṇṇiyaḷ niṉmakaḷkāṇ eṉṟu uṇarttu kiṉṟaṉar. 3. taṉṉi mīṭpataṟkuc curattaḷavum ōṭivanta vīrac cuṟṟattik kaṇṭapōtu, avvaravip poruṭpaṭuttātu, talivaṉip paṟṟi niṉṟu kaṟput tiṇmiyi veḷippaṭuttu vāḷ eṉavum, veṟuṅkiyōṭu cuṟṟattār mīḷtaliṉ avaḷ kaṟpi ūr aṟiyum eṉavum tolkāppiyar kaḷavukkaṟpi aṟivuṟuttuvar. ātaliṉ oḻukkakkaṟpu eññāṉṟum uḷatu. ataṉi īṇṭup pēcātu, tirumaṇa vāḻkki eṉṉum kaṟpuk kikōḷpaṟṟi ārāyvōm.
kaṟpu muṟikaḷ
tamiḻc camutāyattil tirumaṇak kaṟpu muṟikaḷ vanta varalāṟṟi ārāyiṉ, cila maṟivuk kūṟukaḷ pulaṉākum. camutāyattiṉ kuṟikkōḷum, ataṉ ceyalvaḻiyum, vaḻi ituveṉak kāṭṭiya aṟivuk kūrvum viḷaṅkum. camutāyam marapup paṟṟuṭiyatu, marapiṉip putukkik koḷḷum vaḻiyaṟivum uṭiyatu eṉpatellām teḷivupaṭum.  
kaṟpeṉap paṭuvatu karaṇamoṭu puṇarak
koḷaṟkuri marapiṟ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyik
koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppakkoḷ vatuvē. (tol. 1087)
koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē
puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāli yāṉa.	(tol. 1088)
ivi tolkāppiyak kaṟpiyaliṉ mutaliraṇṭu cūttiraṅkaḷ. karaṇam, koṭuppak koḷḷutal iv viraṇṭum kaṟpiṉ pakutikaḷeṉa mutal nūṟpāvāl aṟikiṉṟōm. karaṇamoṭu puṇara marapiṉōr koṭuppar eṉpataṉāl, ak karaṇattic ceyvār marapiṉōrē eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. koṭuppōr illāmalum irukkalām, vēṇṭuvatu karaṇamē eṉat tirumaṇa muṟiyil, karaṇattiṉ (caṭaṅkiṉ) iṉṟiyami yāmiyi aṭutta nūṟpā valiyuṟuttuvatu kāṇka. koṭup pōr illā iṭam etu? atu uṭaṉ pōkiya iṭam eṉavum, uṭaṉ pōkkiṭattut tirumaṇam koḷḷiṉ, koṭuppōr ilar eṉpataṟkākak karaṇatti viṭaṟka. yārikkoṇ ṭēṉum karaṇam peṟa vēṇṭum eṉavum innūṟpā kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu. tirumaṇak kaṟpiṉ iṉṟiyamiyāta ōrmuṟi karaṇamām eṉpatu teḷivu. kaṟpiṟkuk karaṇa nikaḻcci orutaliyāyiṟṟu (tol. 1088) eṉpatu iḷampūraṇa nacciṉārkkiṉiyaṅkaḷ. marapiṉōr koṭuppatilum, karaṇam mēmpaṭṭatu eṉṟa kuṟippu karaṇamoṭu puṇara eṉṉum oṭu urupiṉālum peṟappaṭum (tol. 574). mikat tolpaḻaṅ kālattuk koṭikkuriya marapiṉōr koṭuttal eṉṟa oṉṟē tamiḻiṉattiṉ maṇamuṟiyāka iruntatu; piṉṉar karaṇam (caṭaṅku) eṉṟa oṉṟu uṭaṉ cērntatu; maṇamuṟikaḷ iraṇṭāyiṉa; muṉṉar irunta koṭuppumuṟi piṟpaṭap piṉṉarc cērnta karaṇamuṟi valuppaṭṭatu; atuvē pōtiya muṟiyeṉṟukūṭa āyiṟṟu eṉṟiṉiya varaṉmuṟi kaḷi nām aṟiyat tolkāppiyam utavukiṉṟatu. karaṇam  (caṭaṅku) ēṉ vantatu? ataṉ ēṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa?
poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar       
aiyar yāttaṉar karaṇam eṉpa	(tol. 1090)
ittolkāppiyap pāviṟku vēṟupaṭṭa urikaḷum karutturikaḷum palkiyuḷḷaṉa. eṇṇuvār tam eṇṇatti innūṟpāvil kāṇa viḻikiṉṟaṉar. 23 eṉakkum avviḻivu uṇṭu. akattiṇiyi ārāyap pukka nāṉ ippāvi ārāyātu viṭutaṟkilli. muṉṉi urikaḷ pōl taḷḷup paṭak kūṭātē eṉṉum oru pēraccam eṉ neñciyum aṟiviyum nalvaḻip paṭuttaliṉ, ollum urikāṇa muyalvaṉ.
poyyum vaḻuvum
poy, vaḻu, karaṇam eṉpataṟkup poruḷ eṉṉa? karaṇam (caṭaṅku) iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉiṉ, ataṉ payaṉ eṉṉi? oruvaṉ oruttiyik kaḷaviṟ kātalittup puṇarntāṉ. piṉpu taṉakkum avaḷukkum toṭarpu illi eṉṟu vañcittuk kūṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ vañcaṉiyik karaṇam taṭukkuṅkol? tirumaṇattiṟku uṭaṉpaṭṭu vantārkkaṉṟē caṭaṅku uṇṭu. uṭaṉpāṭilli, toṭarpilli eṉṟu kūṟiyoḻintakkāl, kaṟpu ētu? karaṇam ētu? ātaliṉ kaḷavu muṟiyil tōṉṟum poyyiyum vaḻuviyum karaṇam taṭukkum eṉṟum, taṭuttaṟkākak karaṇatti amittaṉar eṉṟum moḻivār karuttup poruḷaṟṟatu. maṇamākiyapiṉ, kippiṭitta maṉiviyik kiviṭukiṟāṉ, kātal māṟu kiṉṟāṉ, paratti nāṭukiṉṟāṉ. kaṭṭiya karaṇam avaṉi eṉ ceyyum? ātaliṉ kaṟpukkuppiṉ tirumaṇattiṉpiṉ tōṉṟum poyyiyum vaḻuviyum karaṇam taṭukkum eṉṟalum poruḷaṟṟatu. kātalittāri vañcittalum, maṇantārik kiviṭutalum camutāyattil kāṇum nikaḻccikaḷē. nikaḻātavi eṉpār eḻuttu vañcakar.
tolpukaḻ niṟinta palpūṅ kaḻaṉik
karumpal paṭappip perumpeyark kaḷḷūrt
tirunutaṟ kuṟumakaḷ aṇinalam vavviya
aṟaṉi lāḷaṉ aṟiyēṉ eṉṟa
tiṟaṉil veñcūḷ aṟikari kaṭāay
muṟiyār peruṅkiḷi ceṟiyap paṟṟi
nīṟutalip peyta ñāṉṟi
vīṟucāl aviyat tārppiṉum peritē	(akam. 256)
kaḷḷūril kumarip peṇṇāḷ oruttiyiṉ nalam tuytta oruvaṉ, avaḷ yār nāṉ yār eṉṟu uṟavu poyttuc cūḷum ceytāṉ. ūrccāṉṟōrkaḷ takkapaṭi ucāvi, uṟavuṇṭu eṉpatit teḷintaṉar. kātalaṟam poytta akkayavaṉi marattiṟpiṇittut taliyil nīṟṟiṉik koṭṭiṉar. ūrār aṟiyat taṇṭaṉi ceytaṉar annāḷ eṉṟu, kaṭuvaṉ maḷḷaṉār oruvaṉatu kaḷḷat taṉattiyum, avaṉ camutāyattiṟ paṭṭa pāṭṭiyum uvami vāyilākak kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. iṉṉa aṟamil nikaḻccikaḷ eññāṉṟum naṭakkak kāṇkiṉ ṟōm. ivaṟṟi uyirttaṇṭaṉi taṭukkumē tavirak karaṇac caṭaṅku taṭukkātu.  
tolkāppiyam kūṟum poyyum vaḻuvum kātalar kaḷiṭit tōṉṟuvaṉa eṉṟu nām ituvari karutiyiruk kiṉṟōmē, atu peruntavaṟu. kātalarkaḷuṭiya poyyiyum vaḻuviyum akaṟṟak karaṇam vakukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu karutiyirukkiṟōmē, atuvum tavaṟukāṇ. muṟkūṟi yāṅkuk kaḷavi illiyeṉpāṉiyum, kaṟpik kaṭantoḻuku vāṉiyum tirumaṇac caṭaṅku taṭiceyyātu. ceyyā, ceyyamāṭṭāc ceyalik karaṇattiṉ mēliṭṭuk kūṟutal poruntātu. karaṇamāvatu kaḷaviṉ vaḻivanta kātalarkkum, kaḷaviṉ vaḻi vārāk kātalarkkum, aḵtāvatu tirumaṇam pukum ellārkkum ceyyappaṭuvatu. atu tirumaṇak kālattuc ceyyum caṭaṅku. poyyum vaḻuvum illāta āṇ peṇ kaṭkē tirumaṇattaṉṟu caṭaṅku nikaḻkiṉṟatu eṉpati niṉittukkoḷka. ulakattuk kātaṟ piḻipāṭukaḷ pala iruntālum, tamiḻ akattiṇiyum akavilakkiyaṅkaḷum aṉpiṟ kūṭiya kātalarkaḷiyē eṭuttukkoḷkiṉṟaṉa, toṭukkiṉṟaṉa eṉpataṉiyum niṉika. aṉpōṭu puṇarnta aintiṇi (1037). oṉṟi uyarnta pālatu āṇiyiṉ otta kiḻavaṉum kiḻattiyum (1038) eṉat tolkāppiyar akattiṇik kātalarkaḷiṉ aṉpuk kiṭakkiyip pulap paṭuttuvar. aintiṇik kātaṟ poruḷiṉ aṟivuṭi amippiṉi aṭuttuvarum iyalil viḷaṅkak kāṇalām.
VIII
karaṇa varalāṟu
tirumaṇattaṉṟu karaṇam nikaḻum eṉṟu collum pōtu, karaṇam āṇukkum uṇṭu, peṇṇukkum uṇṭu eṉpatupōṟ paṭiṉum, meyyāka nōkkiṉ, karaṇattukku uriyāḷ peṇṇē eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum. tamiḻc camutāya vaḻakkum ilakkiya vaḻakkum kāṭṭum neṟiyitu. iṉṟu perumpāṅku kāṇattakum marapu itu. tirumaṇa nāḷaṉṟu peṇṇukkuk karaṇam ēṉ? peṟṟōr tam makaḷi maṇañ ceytu koṭuttiṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟu koḷvōm. avaḷ maṉiviyāṉāḷ eṉpatu tirumaṇattik kaṇṭārkkum kēṭṭārkkumē teriyavarum. palar aṟitaṟku iṭaṉilli. aṇimiyil maṇam koṇṭa oru peṇṇukkum, maṇap pakkuvam koṇṭa oru peṇṇukkum eṉṉa puṟa vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu? kaḷavu neṟiyip pōṟṟi uṭaṉpaṭṭatu tamiḻc camutāyam. nerunaṟkumari iṉṟu maṉiviyāvāḷ. ataṟku ōr maṇakkuṟi iṉṟeṉiṉ, nāḷi avaḷmēl oru nalliḷi ñaṉukkum iyalpākak kātal ōṭalāmaṉṟō? ivaḷ oruvaṉukku uriyaḷāyiṉāḷ. iṉi yār eṇṇutaṟkum uriyāḷilli eṉṟu caṭṭeṉa aṟitaṟku ētuvāka oru puṟattōṟṟattic camutāyam aḷikkumēl piṟaṉmaṉi nayanta peruṅ kuṟṟattiṟku iḷiñar āḷākāraṉṟē.
ēku nalvaḻi yalvaḻi yeṉmaṉam
āku mōvataṟ kākiya kāraṇam    
pāku pōlmoḻip pintoṭi kaṉṉiyē
ākum vēṟitaṟ kiyuṟa villiyē	(pāla. mitili)
eṉ uḷḷam tīvaḻic cellātu, avvaḷavu tūymiyuṭiyatu. eṉ nalluḷḷam ivaḷmēl ōṭiyamiyiṉ, ivaḷ kaṉṉitāṉ (piṟaruṭiya maṉiviyilli). aiyappaṭavēṇṭām eṉṟu, irāmaṉ mīṇṭum cītiyiṉmēl maṉañ celuttiṉāṉ eṉak kampar tamiḻc camutāya oḻuṅki uṭkoṇṭu pāṭutali oppu nōkkuka.
kuṟṟattiṟku iṭaṅkoṭuttuviṭṭut taṇṭikkap puṟap paṭutal camutāyattiṟkum aracukkum aṟivuṭimiyākātu. kuṟṟa vaḻiyi aṭittu etiratāk kākkum aṟivuṭimiyē aḻakākum. kaṉṉimi kaḻiyāk kumariyōṭu kaṉṉimi akaṉṟa iḷamaṉivikkut tōṟṟaravil paṇṭu evvēṟupāṭum illi. iv vēmāṟṟa niliyip poy eṉat tolkāppiyar kuṟippar. puṟavēṟupāṭiṉmi palarkku mayakkañ ceytaliṉ poyyeṉappaṭṭatu. maṇa aṭiyāḷam illā maṉiviyi iḷantōṟṟattāl kumariyeṉak koṇṭu uḷḷam pōkkiya naliḷiñaṉ, avaḷi nōkkavum kaḷikkavum uriyāṭavum ceyal kāṇavum muṟpaṭuvāṉ. ēmāṟu tōṟṟattāl mēṟkoḷḷum ikkātal muyaṟcikaḷi vaḻu eṉat tolkāppiyar kuṟippar. cila iṭaṅkaḷil kātal muyaṟciyiṉ toṭakkattilēyē, ivaḷ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṉum uṇmi pulaṉākiyirukkum. aṟiyā iḷiñaṉ ceytiyaṟintu kātali aṭakkik koṇṭiruppāṉ! puṇarcci nimittattiṟku uriya ceyal muṟṟum vari, ōrōviṭaṅkaḷil uṇmic ceyti veḷippaṭātu iruntiruttalum kūṭum. uṇmi uṇartta vanta kūṟukaḷi, vēṭki mayakkattāl oruvaṉ taṉakku naṉmi uṇartta vanta kūṟukaḷākat tiripupaṭak koṇṭiruttalum kūṭum. poyyāṉa kumarit tōṟṟattik kaṇṭa oruvaṉ uḷḷattaḷavil kātali aṭakkik koṇṭa poḻutu, ataṉic camutāyam uṇara vāyppilli. aṭakkik koḷḷātu, eṇṇattip perukki, iṇi viḻiccu naccik kumariyaṉiyāḷik koḷḷa muntuṟum pōtu, camutāyam ivaṉatu kātal vaḻu eṉpataṉiyum, ivvaḻuviṟku ivaḷ poyttōṟṟam-kumariyōṭu vēṟupāṭiṉmi-kāraṇam eṉpataṉiyum kāraṇa kāriyattōṭu aṟintu koḷkiṉṟatu. kātal vaḻuppaṭṭāṉik kuṟi kūṟutaṟkō, vaḻuvukkuk kaḷamākiya iḷamaṉi yāḷik kuṟi kūṟutaṟkō iṭaṉilli. kuṟi camutāyattiṉ pālatu.
karaṇat tōṟṟam
eccamutāyattiṉ paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum kuṟivaṟṟa niṟiyuṭiyaṉa valla eṉiṉum, āciriyaṉ pōlak kuṟi kaḷiya vēṇṭum eṉṟa cennōkkamum, kuṟipāṭukaḷik kūrntu kāṇum aṟivumuṉippum, vaḷarum camutāyattiṟku vēṇṭiyavi. tiruttik koḷḷum camutāyattil kuṟṟap perukkattiṟkup paṟṟukkōṭu illi. paṇṭit tamiḻc camutāyattil, ataṉ iyakkatti uṟṟunōkki ōrntu āvaṉa ceyyum iṉap periyavarkaḷ iruntaṉar. ivarkaḷi aiyar eṉpar tolkāppiyar. nāṭu muḻuvatum naṉku matitta periyōravi tamiḻ nilattu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉpataṟku vēṟu kari vēṇṭā. pulavarellām oppiya akattiṇip paṭippē pōtumāṉatu. mēṟkūṟiya poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṟpāṭē, camutāyattiṉ tirumaṇamuṟik kuṟipāṭu iṉac cāṉṟōrkaṭkup pulaṉāyiṟṟu. vaḻuviṟkuk kāraṇam poy. vaḻuvakala vēṇṭiṉ poy akalavēṇṭum. maṇappakkuvam eytiyirukkum peṇṇukkum, pakkuvam vantu maṇam eytiya peṇṇukkum puṟattōṟṟattil yārum kāṇa aṭiyāḷam vēṇṭum eṉṟu cāṉṟōrkaṭkup paṭṭatu. vēṟupāṭu kāṭṭum karaṇam-caṭaṅku-vakuttaṉar. poyyum vaḻuvum eṉṟa tolkāppiyap pāviṉ karuttu ituvē. peṇṇiṉ kaṟpu evvakiyāṉum ūṟupaṭak kūṭātu. ūṟupaṭa viṭalākātu eṉṟu kaṇṇum karuttumāy nāṭṭañ celuttiya tamiḻc camutāyattiṉ maṉavōṭṭam peṟappaṭum. 
maṇṭiṇi ñālattu maḻivaḷan tarūum
peṇṭi rāyiṉ piṟarneñcu pukāar
eṉṟu maṇimēkalic cāttaṉār kaṟpukkuk kaṭiya varampu ceyvar. maṉivikku maṇappoṟi illiyeṉiṉ, tavaṟākap piṟar neñcu pukumnili ēṟpaṭumaṉṟō? peṟṟōriṉ vaṟumiyāl piḷḷikaḷ vāṭutalpōlac camutāyakkuṟi nallavarkaḷiyum kuṟiyākkum. poyyum vaḻuvum tōṉṟiya piṉṉar eṉṉum varalāṟṟu nūṟpāviṟkum, ivviraṇṭum kātalarkaḷiṭit tōṉṟiyavi eṉṟu ellōrum uri ceyvar. atu uriyākāmi mēli viḷakkattāl aṟintīr. ivi akattiṇik kātalarkaḷiṭit tōṉṟuvaṉa valla eṉṟu teḷivāka niṉittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. koṭikkuri marapiṉōr koṭuppak koḷḷal eṉṉum oru maṇaneṟiyāl, poyyum vaḻuvum (uḷa eṉṟu) veḷippaṭi yāka aṟinta piṉṉar - aḵtāvatu poyyum vaḻuvum iṉac cāṉṟōrukku veḷippaṭṭa piṉṉē-avarkaḷ avaṟṟi yoḻittataṟkuk karaṇam eṉṉum maṟṟoru neṟiyi vakuttut tantaṉar eṉpatuvē ap pāviṉ poruḷām. tōṉṟiya piṉṉar eṉpataṉ poruḷik kūrntu koḷka, tōṉṟal - veḷippaṭutal.
karaṇakkuṟi
tirumaṇattiṟkuk karaṇa vaḻakku iṉṟiyamiyātatu eṉṟa uṇmi tolkāppiyar kālattukku muṉṉarē uṇarap paṭṭatu. eṉiṉum, karaṇakkuṟi allatu caṭaṅkaṭiyāḷam kālantōṟum tamiḻakattu vēṟupaṭṭirukkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. iññāṉṟu nāṭu muḻuvatum tāli potuvaṭiyāḷamāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. cila pakutikaḷil tāliyuṭaṉ miñci eṉṉum kālviral mōtiraṅkaḷum maṇakkuṟiyāka uḷa. im mōtiraṅkaḷ kāliṉ iraṇṭāvatu viralil māṭṭappaṭupavi. kaḻuttiṟ kaṭṭum tāli aṭimikkuṟi eṉavum, kaṇavaṉukku maṉivi aṭimi eṉpatuvē tāliyiṉ karuttu eṉavum oppāṭikaḷ karaṇak kuṟikku viḷakkañ ceykuvar. aṭimik karuttāyiṉ perum piḻitāṉ; tāli akaṟṟappaṭa vēṇṭiyatutāṉ. tāliyāvatu maṇavaṇi. maṇaṅ kāṭṭi, piṟarkku vēṇṭā eṇṇam tōṉṟātu taṭukkum aṟivaṇi. iṉṟiya karuttukkōḷ poruntātu eṉpatu karaṇam tōṉṟiya varalāṟṟāl teḷiyalām. paṇṭic camutāyattil peṇṇiṉattukku urimiyiruntatā? otta matippu iruntatā? ivviṉākkaḷukku viriviṭikūṟa iḵtu iṭamilli.
peṇṇurimi
palvēṟu nakaraṅkaḷiṉ kūṟukaḷiyum iṉaṅkaḷiṉ vaḻakkāṟukaḷiyum aṟikiṉṟōm. aṟiviyal vēkam ellārkkum potuvuṇarvi ūṭṭukiṉṟatu. inniliyil iṉṟu urimikkum ottalukkum ulakaḷavāl virinta karuttuk koṇṭu varukiṉṟōm. ikkaruttu iṉṉum vaḷaravuṅ kūṭum. paṇṭit tamiḻc camutāya aḷavil nōkkiṉ peṇ kaṟkum urimiyum, kavipāṭum urimiyum, kātalurimiyum, kātaṟ kaḷavu ceyyum urimiyum, kātalaṉi iṭitturikkum urimiyum, illaṟat toḻilurimiyum perumiyum pukaḻum ellām āṭavarkku nikarākap peṟṟiruntāḷ eṉpatu neṟṟit tilakam (veḷippaṭi). camutāyattiṉ nākarikatti ataṉ moḻic coṟkaḷil kaṇṭu koḷḷalām. moḻi nākarikatti vañcikkātu. talivaṉ talivi, kātalaṉ kātali, kiḻavaṉ kiḻatti eṉṉum pālnikar coṟkaḷ tamiḻc camutāya moḻiyil uḷa. piṟappē kuṭimi āṇmi āṇṭu (1218) eṉa irupālārkkum oppiṉatu vakikaḷit tolkāppiyar ēṟṟat tāḻviṉṟic cuṭṭuvar. ivi ottiruntālallatu aṟivuṭiyārkaṭ kāmakkuṟipput tōṉṟa māṭṭā eṉṟu uriviḷakkam ceykuvar pērāciriyar. iṉpa nukarccikkē ēṟpa oppu vēṇṭum eṉpatu avar urittuṇipu. iru pērāṇmi ceyta pūcal (kuṟun. 43) eṉṟu caṅkakāla auviyār pāṭutaliṉ, āṇmi peṇṇukkum uṇṭu eṉavum, eṇṇattit tiṟampaṭa āḷum matuki iru pālārkkum uriyatē eṉavum paṇṭit tamiḻc camutāya maṉap pōkki aṟintu koḷkiṉṟōm.  
akattil, kuṭumpavulakil taliviyē iṟimi uṭiyāḷ; avaḷē vēntāvāḷ. maṉivi, illāḷ eṉṉum vīṭṭuc coṟkaḷ avaḷukkē uriyavi. ivaṟṟiṟku nikarāṉa āṇpāṟ kiḷavikaḷ illāmi niṉiyattakum. tirumaṇam eṉpatu talivaṉ taṉ akavāḻviṉ urimiyellām maṉivikku vaḻaṅkum āvaṇakkaḷari. puṟattoḻukkam kaṇṭa talivaṉi illil nuḻiyaṟka eṉṟu iṭittut tiruttum collurimiyum uṭiyavaḷ talivi. itaṉi añca vanta urimi (1091) eṉpar tolkāppiyar. vāyil vēṇṭal, vāyil maṟuttal, vāyil nērtal eṉṟa tuṟikaḷellām kaṟpuṭiya maṉiviyiṉ urimiyiṟ piṟantavi. ātaliṉ nam camutāyattil, peṇṇiṟku matippuk kuṟivu eṉavum, karaṇakkuṟi-aḵtāvatu tirumaṇam kāṭṭum aṭiyāḷam - aṭimip poṟi eṉavum karuttuppaṭutal eṉṉāṉum poruntātu. kātalarkaḷiṉ kaḷḷattaṉattiyum kiviṭutali yum taṭuttaṟkuc ceyyappaṭuvatu karaṇam eṉṟalum poruntavē poruntātu.
tirumaṇac caṭaṅku kaṟpākātu: kaṟpip piṟappikkātu. kātaṟkaḷavu naṭattum kumari naṅkikku yāy eṉṟu peyar koṭukkiṉṟār ōrampōkiyār (aiṅ. 6-10) vēṭṭōḷē yāyē nāḷi maṇantu maṉiviyāy makappeṟṟut tāyāvāḷ eṉpatil taṭiyeṉṉō? īṇṭut taliviyi yāy eṉṟatu etirppaṭṭa ñāṉṟē kaṟpup pūṇṭoḻukukiṉṟa ciṟappi nōkki eṉpar aiṅkuṟunūṟṟiṉ mutaluriyāciriyar. ivvuri urikaḷuḷ nallatu, ciṟantatu. kaṟpoḻukkam tirumaṇattāl uṇṭāvatilli. uriya kātalaṉi etirppaṭṭa appoḻutē avaḷ avvoḻukkattalippaṭṭāḷ eṉṟu ivvuri aṟivikkiṉṟatu. karaṇam (caṭaṅku) eṉpatu tirumaṇa aṭiyāḷam; peṇṇorutti kaṉṉimi kaḻintu maṉivimi pukkāḷ eṉpataṉik kāṭṭum maṉiyaṇi eṉpatu eṉ tuṇipu.

IX
cilampu kaḻi nōṉpu
caṅka kālattu vaḻakkil irunta karaṇam yātu? aṉṟu karaṇam iruntatu, atu vēṇṭuvatu eṉac camutāya marapik kūṟiṉārēyaṉṟi, naṭimuṟiyil mēṟkoṇṭa karaṇakkuṟi ituveṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟiṉārallar. avar kūṟāmikkuk kāraṇam tiṇitōṟum kulantōṟum vēṟu vēṟu karaṇa aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām. taṟkālattuk karaṇak kuṟiyik kūṟiṉ, itutāṉ kuṟiyeṉa vaḻivaḻi makkaḷ aṭimippaṭuvar. atu vēṇṭā; putiya karaṇak kuṟikaḷ kālattiṟ kēṟpak koḷḷiṉum tavaṟilli eṉṟu āciriyar karutiyirukkalām. karaṇam ituveṉak kūṟātu, karaṇam vēṇṭum eṉṟu camutāya ilakkaṇatti maṭṭum valiyuṟuttuvar tolkāppiyar. caṅkap paṉuvalkaḷik koṇṭu paṇṭu nilaviya karaṇakkuṟikaḷi nām uyttuṇara muṭiyum. peṇ kuḻantikaḷ kuḻavimitoṭṭē kāliṟ cilampu aṇiyum vaḻakkam muṉpu iyalpāka iruntatu eṉṟu pala pāṭaṟ kuṟippukkaḷ cuṭṭukiṉṟaṉa.
cīrkeḻu viyaṉakarc cilampunaka iyali
ōri yāyamoṭu pantuciṟi teṟiyiṉum,
vārā yōveṉ ṟēttip pērilip
pakaṉṟi vāṉmalar paṉiniṟin tatupōl
pālpey vaḷḷam cālki paṟṟi
eṉpā ṭuṇṭaṉi yāyiṉ orukāl
nunti pāṭum uṇṇeṉ ṟūṭṭip
piṟantataṟ koṇṭum ciṟantavi ceytuyāṉ
nalampuṉin teṭuttaveṉ polantoṭik kuṟumakaḷ	(akam. 219)
taṉ periya vīṭṭiṉkaṇ kāṟcilampu olippac ciṟu tōḻiyaroṭu pantu viḷiyāṭuvāḷ. ciṟitu āṭiṉum, kāl nōvumē, nā vēṭkumē eṉṟu aḻippēṉ. aḻittu, kiṇṇam niṟinta pāli eṉakkāka oruvāy uṇṭāy, uṉ tantikkākavum oruvāy kuṭippāy eṉṟu collil mayakkip pālūṭṭip piṟanta nāḷ mutal vaḷarttēṉ eṉa ivvāṟu paruvakālattu uṭaṉpōya makaḷukku avaḷtaṉ maḻalik kālattuk kāṭṭiya aṉpiṉit tāy eṇṇip pārkkiṉṟāḷ. pāluṇ paruvattuc cilampu iṭum vaḻakkāṟu itaṉāṟ peṟappaṭum.
talivaṉ taṉ puṇarcci vēṭkiyi veḷippaṭac colla, avaṉ viḻivukku icintu, maḻi peyyum naṭu yāmattut talivi iravukkaḷam varukiṉṟāḷ. celavu varavi vīṭṭār taṉ cilampiṉ ōciyāl aṟintuviṭalākātē eṉṟu ataṉ aravattik kaṭṭi oṭukki vantu celkiṉṟāḷ:
añcilampu oṭukki añciṉaḷ vantu
tuñcūr yāmattu muyaṅkiṉaḷ peyarvōḷ 	(akam. 198)
eṉṟu talivaṉ kātaliyiṉ aṉpiyum aṟiviyum viyakkiṉṟāṉ.24 uṭaṉvarum kātaṟ kaḷavarkaḷi vaḻiyiṭik kaṇṭār villēntiya ivaṉ kālil vīrakkaḻalkaḷ uḷa; vaḷiyal aṇinta ivaḷ mellaṭikaḷil cilampukaḷ uḷa (villōṉ kālaṉa kaḻalē; toṭiyōḷ mellaṭi mēlavum cilampē - kuṟun. 7) eṉṟu kālaṇi pārttu, iṉṉum tirumaṇam koḷḷātavarkaḷ eṉat tuṇikiṉṟaṉar. in nallōr yārkol, pēṇattakkavarkaḷ eṉṟu taṇṇaḷik kiṉṟaṉar. nakkaṇṇiyār āmūr mallaṉi aṭṭu niṉṟa kōpperu naṟkiḷḷiyik kāmuṟṟāḷ. akkiḷḷi vēṟṟu nāṭṭu vēṟṟūriṉaṉ. avaṉatu maṟpōr veṉṟiyiṉik kaṇkuḷirppak kāṇum iṉiya vāyppu avaṭkuk kiṭittatu. makiḻcciyil añcilampu olippa ōṭiṉēṉ (puṟam. 85) eṉṟu nakkaṇṇiyārē pāṭukiṉṟār. kāliṟ cilampu uṭiyāḷ kumari eṉṟu oru camutāya uṇmiyi aṟiyavum tuṇiyavum immēṟkōḷkaḷ kariyāvaṉa.
kumarikkuc cilampu aṭiyāḷam eṉpataṉāl, kumarik kaḻivukkuc cilampuk kaḻivu vēṇṭappaṭum. eṉavē tiru maṇattiṟ cilampukaḻi nōṉpu oru karaṇamāyiṟṟu, caṭaṅkāyiṟṟu, iṉṟiyamiyāta viḻāvāyiṟṟu. itu caṅkac camutāya vaḻakku. uṭaṉpōkiya kālattu uṟaviṉarillāp putiyatōr nāṭṭil maṇañ ceytukoṇṭālum, āṇṭum cilampik kaḻaṟṟum caṭaṅku nikaḻum; nikaḻa vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm.  
uḷḷātu kaṉinta muḷḷeyiṟṟut tuvarvāyc
ciṟuvaṉ kaṇṇi cilampu kaḻīiya
aṟiyāt tēettaḷ ākutal koṭitē	(akam. 385)
cilampu kaḻīiya celvam    
piṟaruṇak kaḻintaveṉ ṉāyiḻi yaṭiyē	(naṟ. 275)
taṉ makaḷ ceṉṟa nāṭṭiṟ cilampu kaḻittal koṭitu eṉa oru tāy ciṉakkiṉṟāḷ. cilampu kaḻittal oru celvam eṉṟum, ataṉik kāṇum pēṟṟi īṉṟa tāykku aḷikkātu piṟarkku aḷikkap pōyiṉāḷē eṉṟum oru tāy avalikkiṉṟāḷ. koṭuppōr iṉṟiyum karaṇam uṇṭē, puṇarntuṭaṉ pōkiya kāliyāṉa (1088) eṉṉum tolkāppiya viti naṭimuṟiyil irunta vaḻakku eṉpataṉiyum, avvaḻakkiṉ uṭporuḷiyum mēli viḷakkattāl aṟika.
cilampu aṇinōṉpu eṉa oru viḻāmarapu kāṇappaṭā miyiṉ, peṇ kuḻantikaṭkuc cilampu iṭutal mika iyalpāṉa vaḻakku eṉṟu ūkikkalām. akkuḻanti yaṇiyit tirumaṇattiṟkumuṉ nīkkuvar. nīṅkuṅkāl atu oru viḻāvākak koṇṭāṭappaṭṭuc cilampukaḻi nōṉpu eṉap peyarpeṟum. innōṉpu tirumaṇattiṟkuc ciṟitu muṉṉarttāṉ nikaḻum eṉpataṉi nummaṉic cilampukaḻīi ayariṉum, emmaṉi vatuvi naṉmaṇam kaḻika (aiṅ. 399) eṉṟa naṟṟāy vēṭkiyāl aṟiyalām. mēlum, cilampukaḻi viḻāvi ōriṭattu nikaḻtti, ataṉ piṉṉar (koṭuppak koḷḷal eṉṉum) tirumaṇatti vēṟōriṭattuc ceytal uṇaṭu eṉṟa vaḻakkāṟṟiyum aṟiyalām. kuḻanti mutalē kiṭakkum cilampiṉ nīkkam ivaḷ maṉiviyāṉāḷ eṉpataṉi muḻuc camutāyattiṟkum kāṭṭi viṭum. ivaḷ maṇattiyāṉāḷ eṉpataṉi aṇukik kāliṟ cilampillāmi kaṇṭukūṭat tuṇiyavēṇṭum eṉpatilli. cilampiṉ uḷḷirukkum paralōci kēḷāmiyiṉāl, cēymiyilēyē tuṇintuviṭa iṭamuṇṭu. cilampik kaḻittal, iruntati nīkkutal eṉpatu oru kāraṇamākavum, atuvē tirumaṇattiṉ aṟikuṟiyākavum āyiṟṟu. uḷḷati oḻittal aṭiyāḷamākumā? ākum. iyalpāka vaḷarum talimayiri maḻittal tuṟavuk kōlamākavilliyā?  
X
kūntal malaraṇital
paṇṭi nāḷil vaḻaṅkiya karaṇam oṉṟē; atutāṉum cilampiṉ akaṟci eṉṟu muṭivu kaṭṭutaṟku illi. cilampiṉmi ōr etirmaṟi yaṭiyāḷam. ivaḷ maṇavāṭṭi eṉṟu aṟivippāṉ pala aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷ tiṇikkēṟpa iruntiruttal kūṭum. avi ilakkiyam ēṟātiruttalum kūṭum. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷi nuṇukik kāṇpārkku maṟṟoru aṭiyāḷamum vaḻaṅkiyami teriyavarum. iḵtu uṭaṉ pāṭāṉa aṟikuṟi; peritum vaḻakkil paraviyirunta karaṇam.
taṇkayat tamaṉṟa oṇpūṅ kuvaḷi
arumpalit tiyaṟṟiya curumpār kaṇṇi
piṉṉup puṟantāḻak koṉṉē cūṭṭi
nalvaral iḷamuli nōkki neṭituniṉintu
nillātu peyarntaṉaṉ oruvaṉ ataṟkē...
poṉṉēr nuṇtātu nōkki
eṉṉum nōkkumiv vaḻuṅkal ūrē	(akam. 180)
kuvaḷik kaṇṇiyi eṉ piṉṉiya kūntalil cummā cūṭṭiṉāṉ; vaḷarum mārpi oru nōkku nōkkic ceṉṟu viṭṭāṉ; intac ceyalukkē ūr eṉṉi oruvakiyākap pārkkalāyiṟṟu. puṉṉit tātiṉ niṟattiyum pacali paṭarnta eṉ mēṉi niṟattiyum uṟṟu nōkkiṟṟu eṉat talivi ūralarukkuk kāraṇam urikkiṉṟāḷ. kūntalil pūvik kaṇṭavaḷavilēyē ūrppārvi māṟiviṭṭatē eṉṟu talimakaḷ nontu koḷḷiṉum, oruvaṉōṭu oruttikku uṟavu uṇṭu eṉpataṟku malar pōtumāṉa aṭiyāḷam eṉṟu ūrār tuṇivuṟṟu alar moḻintaṉar eṉṟu nām aṟiya vēṇṭum.
ampal mūtūr alarvāyp peṇṭir
iṉṉā iṉṉuri kēṭṭa ciṉṉāḷ
aṟiyēṉ pōla uyirēṉ
naṟiya nāṟumniṉ katuppeṉ ṟēṉē	(naṟ. 143)
iṉṉāṉuṭaṉ ivar makaḷukkut toṭarpu   eṉṟu eṉ kātu kēṭka ūrppeṇṭukaḷ pēcuvati aṟivēṉ. cila nāṭkaḷākat terintu vittum, teriyātavaḷ pōla oṉṟum kāṭṭik koḷḷātiruntēṉ.  orunāḷ niṉ kūntal naṟumaṇam vīcukiṉṟatē eṉṟu kēṭṭuviṭṭēṉ  eṉṟu tāy taṉ makaḷ uṭaṉpōkiyataṟkut tāṉē kāraṇam eṉac collu kiṉṟāḷ. kumariyar kūḻiyil pūmaṇattaṟku iṭamilli; niṉ kūntal maṇakkiṉṟatē eṉṟu aṉṉi viṉaviyatum kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭuviṭṭatu. iṉi vīṭṭil iruttal poruntātu eṉṟu makaḷ tāykku añci uṭaṉpōyiṉāḷ. iyaṟki vaṉappumikka iṭattil iṉpuṟum talivaṉ kātaliyiṉ karuṅkuḻalil pūṅkottukkaḷic cūṭi aṇiceytu makiḻvāṉ. vīṭu cellumpōtu talimakaḷ pūvik kūntaliliruntu akaṟṟi viṭuvāḷ. malari eṭutteṟintu viṭṭālum, malar irunta kūntal maṇakkumaṉṟō? ātaliṉ tāy makaḷiṉ kūntalil malarip pārkkā viṭṭālum, ataṉ maṇatti aṟintu, niṉ katuppu naṟiya nāṟum eṉṟu viṉaviṉāḷ eṉak koḷka.
kāṭṭik koṭutta mulli
pulliṉat tāya makaṉcūṭi vantatōr
mulli yorukāḻum kaṇṇiyum melliyāl
kūntaluṭ peytu muṭittēṉmaṉ tōḻi yāy
veṇṇey urii viritta katuppōṭē
aṉṉiyum attaṉum illarā yāynāṇa
aṉṉimuṉ vīḻntaṉṟap pū   	(kali. 115)
iṭimakaṉ cūṭiya mullit toṭiyiyum mullic carattiyum puṟattē teriyātapaṭi eṉ kūntalukku uḷḷē vittu muṭittēṉ; ataṉāl eṉṉa? niṉiviṉṟi veṇṇeyit taṭavavē, kūntal virintu uḷḷirunta pū aṉṉikku muṉṉē vīḻntatu. avaḷ nāṇiṉāḷ. yātum viṉavavilli; ciṉakkavum illi. ēṉ? avaḷukku oṉṟum puriyavilli. neruppit toṭṭavarpōlak kiyi vitirvitirttu, avviṭattu nillātē pōyviṭṭāḷ. maṟittup putitākak kaṭkuṭittavaṉ meymaṟantu vāyāl uṇmiyi uḷaṟutal pōla, eṉ nili yāyiṟṟu eṉa oru kaḷavu naṅki, pūvāl kaḷavu veḷippaṭṭuviṭṭatu eṉṟum, mēlviḷivu yātākumō eṉṟum naṭuṅkukiṉṟāḷ. kūntaluḷ peytu muṭittēṉeṉṟa uḷ urupunaṭiyāl kātalaṉ cūṭiya pūmēl avaḷukku irunta kātalum, ataṉi maṇattālum aṉṉi aṟiyātapaṭi oḷittuc ceṟittu vittukkoḷḷum avaḷ aṟivuṭimiyum viḷaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṉṉi muṉ vīḻntaṉṟu appū eṉa muṉ urupāṟ colluṅkāli, yārukku maṟikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉittēṉ. avarukku muṉṉē antappū viḻuntatē eṉṟa avaḷ naṭukkamum accamum tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. kūntaliṉ uḷḷē pūvi maṟippāṉēṉ? atu aṉṉi muṉ vīḻntakkāl añcutalēṉ?
kumari malaraṇiyāmi
paṇṭit tamiḻk kaṉṉi malarcūṭāḷ eṉpatum, malar cūṭiṉ kaṉṉiyākāḷ, oruvaṉi varittāḷ eṉpatum nam paḻañ camutāya vaḻakku. ikkaruttu kaṟṟārkku iṉṟu viyappākavum puraṭciyākavum tōṉṟum, tōṉṟakkūṭum. ivvaḻakkam innāḷ tamiḻakattu illāmiyum, irukka iyalāmiyum kaṇṭu palar malikkavum malippar. teḷiviṟku iṉṉum cila cāṉṟukaḷ kāṇpōm.
iraṇṭaṟi kaḷvinaṅ kāta  lōḷē
muraṇkoḷ tuppiṟ cevvēl maliyaṉ
muḷḷūrk kāṉam nāṟa vantu
naḷḷeṉ kaṅkul nammō raṉṉaḷ; 
kūntal vēynta viravumalar utirttuc
cāntuḷar naṟuṅkatup peṇṇey nīvi
amarā mukatta ḷākit
tamarō raṉṉaḷ vikaṟi yāṉē 	(kuṟun. 312)
iravukkuṟi vantu cellum talimakaṉ iṉi maṇantu koḷḷa vēṇṭiyatutāṉ eṉṟu taṉṉeñciṟkuk kūṟum karuttu ṭiyatu ippāṭṭu. palamalarkaḷic cūṭik kamakama veṉṟu maṇam parappi iravil vantu muyaṅki eṉṉavaḷā kiṉṟāḷ; piṉpu cūṭiya malarkaḷi utirttuk kalaviyiṟ kalinta kūntalic cīrceytu, yātoru māṟupāṭum tōṉṟātapaṭi, kāliyil taṉ vīṭṭārukkuk kāṭciyaḷikkiṉṟāḷ. eṉakku ēṟpavum taṉ cuṟṟattārukku ēṟpavum iṭamaṟintu naṭantu koḷḷum eṉ kātali, neñcē! et taṉmiyaḷ teriyumā? avaḷ iraṇṭaṟi kaḷvi eṉṟu talivaṉ kātalāḷiṉ aṟivut tiṟatti viyakkiṉṟāṉ. kūntalil peyta malar, tāṉē utiravilli; vēṇṭumeṉṟē utirkkiṉṟāḷ. ēṉ? utirttu eṉṟa piṟaviṉiyi niṉika. kaṉṉiyar malar cūṭuvatu iyalpu vaḻakkāyiṉ, talivaṉ aṇinta malariyum iyalpu vaḻakkup pōlap peṟṟōrkkuk kāṭṭiviṭalāmaṉṟō? aṉṉi muṉ pū viḻuntapōtu talivi avalappaṭa vēṇṭā maṉṟē? aṉṉiyum atircciyuṟa vēṇṭuvatilliyē! aṉpōṭu cūṭiya pūvi accattōṭu niṉintu utirttāḷ eṉiṉ, kumariyar pūvaṇital camutāya vaḻakkiṟku ovvāk kāṭci eṉpatu teḷivu.

mullik kumariyiṉ kavali
peypō taṟiyāttaṉ kūḻiyuḷ ētilāṉ
kipuṉi kaṇṇi muṭittāḷeṉṟu yāykēṭpiṉ 
ceyvati lākumō maṟṟu	(kali. 107)
kumarip peṇṇāḷ oruttiyiṉ kavaliyi im mullikkali uṇarttukiṉṟatu. avaḷ kavarccikkuk kāraṇam eṉkol? mullittiṇiyil ēṟukōḷviḻā naṭantatu. pārvi yāḷarākak kumariyarellām kuḻumiyiruntaṉar. ceviyilē maṟiyuṭiya oru kāḷiyi iṭiyaṉ maṭakkiṉāṉ; aṭakkiṉāṉ. akkāḷi taṉ kompiṉāl avaṉ taliyiṟ cūṭiya mulliccaratti eṭuttu māṭṭikkoṇṭu cuḻaṟṟiyatu. kompu cuḻaṉṟa vēkattil oru mullippū taṉittuc ceṉṟu eṅkō irunta iṭimakaḷiṉ kūḻik kaṟṟiyuḷḷē pōy viḻuntatu - (appū vantu eṉ kūḻiyuḷ vīḻntaṉṟu). iḻanta poruḷ etirpārātu kiṭippiṉ, āciyōṭu ataṉip pōṟṟikkoḷvatupōla, tāṉē vantu kūntalil viḻunta pūvik kīḻē naḻuvi viḻātapaṭi anta āymakaḷ taliyil muṭittuk koṇṭaṉaḷ. viḻāviṟku vanta peṇkaḷ cilar kaṇṇilēṉum in nikaḻcci paṭṭiruttal kūṭum. kātaliṟ kaṇṭatik kāṇātārkkup pōyk kūṟiṉālallatu cila kaṇkaḷukkut tūkkam vantoḻiyā; ātaliṉ, mullippūvi muṭittuk koṇṭami kaṇṭa mātarkaḷ, tam vīṭṭukku nērē cellātu avaḷ aṉṉikkuc ceṉṟu alar colli yiruttal kūṭum. inniliyil eṉṉa ceyvēṉ? eṅṅaṉam tāyik kāṇpēṉ? kēṭṭāl, etaṉi maṟumoḻivēṉ eṉṟu tuṭikkiṉṟāḷ mullimuṭitta kūntali. ivaḷ kūḻik kūntal ituvari malar peytu aṟiyātatu. oru pūmēl ācikoṇṭu ivaḷ tāṉē cūṭikkoṇṭālum, atuvē tavaṟām. ayalāṉ kitoṭṭut taliyil vitta kaṇṇip pū vantu viḻuntateṉiṉ, ēṟiṭṭup pārātu eṭuttu eṟiya vēṇṭiyatiruppa, eṉ makaḷ ataṉi viḻintu muṭittup potintu koṇṭāḷāyiṉ, akkuṟṟam poṟukkun tarattatō? eṉṟellām tāy kāraṇatti aṭukki niṉittu aṭaṅkāc ciṉaṅkoṇṭāl, eṉceyvōm? eṉak kaṟpaṉi ceytu kavalkiṉṟāḷ mullik kuḻali.    
avaḷ kavali kiṭakka; akkavali namakku aṟivippatu eṉṉa? peypōtu aṟiyāt taṉ kūḻi eṉṟa toṭariṉ karuttu yātu? mayirukku maṇantarum pū muṭittal vēṇṭum eṉṟu aṟiyātavaḷ eṉpatu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar uri viḷakkam. pū muṭittup paḻakkamillātavaḷ eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. peypōtu aṟiyā eṉpataṟku muṭikkum (cūṭutaṟ kuriya) pū itu eṉṟu aṟiyāta eṉpatu avaruri. ivvuriyik kāṭṭilum pōtupeytali (cūṭutali) aṟiyāta eṉṟu urippatuvē poruntum. ōōtal vēṇṭum oḷi māḻkum ceyviṉi(953) eṉṉum kuṟaḷ naṭiyōṭu peypōtu eṉṟa naṭiyi oppiṭuka. uri etuvāyiṉum kumarikkoṇṭi pū maṇam aṟiyātatu eṉpatuvē poruḷ.
kūḻik kūntal   
kūntalik kuṟikkum palavakic coṟkaḷuḷ kūḻi eṉpatu oṉṟu. iccol oru poruḷiṉ iṟutiniliyi, iṟutippakutiyiṉ kuṟiniliyik kuṟikkum.25 iḷaṅkumari yiṉ talimayir nīṇṭa vaḷarcci peṟṟiruppatilli. iṉṉum vaḷara vēṇṭum kuṟiyuṭiyatu. 26 ātaliṉ kūḻik kiḷaviyik kumariyar kūntalukkē āṭciceyyum ilakkiya vaḻakkik kāṇkiṉṟōm. (kuṟun. 113; naṟ. 140; akam. 315; kali. 107). muṭittaṟku vēṇṭum kūntal nīṭci maṉivip paruvattē vaḷarumātaliṉ, pū aṇiyum vaḻakkam tirumaṇam toṭaṅki mēṟkoḷḷappaṭuvatu eṉak koḷḷalām. kumarip paruvattuk kūḻik kūntalic ceviliyar eṇṇey taṭavi vārip piṉṉi viṭumaḷavē ceykuvar. piṉṉiya kūntalil pū aṇivatilli.
piṉṉuviṭ ṭiruḷiya aimpāl	(kali. 59)
maṇṇi mācaṟṟa niṉkūḻi	(kali. 107)
piṉṉuviṭu neṟiyiṟ kiḷiiya kūntalaḷ	(akam. 158)
kūntal vāri nucuppivan tōmpiya
nalamputaṉi yutavi    	(akam. 195)
iṉiya pāṭalaṭikaḷ kumariyar kūntaliṉ oppaṉiyil pū illāmikkuc cāṉṟukaḷām. kaṉṉiyar pūvaṇital ākātu eṉṟu tamiḻc camutāyam koṇṭa muṟikkuk kāraṇam eṉṉa? polivuṭiyatu, kaṇṇukkiṉiyatu, naṭpāṭat tuṇiyāvatu, kikku aḻakiyatu, maṇam potintatu, maṉattik kiḷaṟuvatu, kātalukku nākarikat tūtāvatu; aṉiya peruñ ciṟappum vāynta oru ciṟu poruḷ pū; pūvaṉiyar pūppeytiya kumariyar.
kōṭal etirmukip pacuvī mulli
nāṟitaḻk kuvaḷiyo ṭiṭippaṭa virii
aitutoṭi māṇṭa kōti pōla
naṟiya nallōḷ mēṉi
muṟiyiṉum vāyvatu muyaṅkaṟkum iṉitē	(kuṟun. 32)
vēṅkiyum kāntaḷum nāṟi
āmpal malariṉum tāṉtaṇ ṇiyaḷē	(kuṟun. 84)
kātaṟkaṉṉi mulliyum kuvaḷiyum vēṅkiyum kāntaḷum āmpalum pōlpavaḷ eṉavum, aimmiyum naṟumiyum iṉimiyum taṉmiyum vāynta meṉmiyaḷ eṉavum, pūvākavum pūviṉ taṉmiyaṉavāka pōṟṟap paṭukiṉṟāḷ. illak kumariyar pūccūṭi maṇam parappip piṟarik kavaravēṇṭum eṉpatilli; kavarutalum kūṭātu, attakiya ceypuṉi kavarcci kaṟpu neṟikku ovvātu. kātal neṟiyi vaḷarkkātu eṉṟellām tamiḻ mūtaṟiñarkaḷ kaṇṭaṉar; kumariyar pūvaṇitalik kaṭintaṉar eṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum.
XI
pūvaṇiyum urimi
kumariyum pūvaṇiyum urimiyit tirumaṇanāḷ mutal peṟukiṉṟaṉar. ivaḷ illaṟatti, maṉiviyāṉāḷ eṉpataṟkup pūkkaraṇam ceyyappaṭum. ikkaraṇattit talivaṉ ceyvāṉ eṉṟu aṟikiṉṟōm. cenniṟa vēṅkip pūviṉmēl irunta mayil nakiyaṇinta peṇ pōlak kāṭciyaḷikkum nāṭṭavaṉē, nallatu ceytāy; niṉ kuṭumpam vāḻka. tirumaṇattik koṇṭāṭumpaṭi piṉṉiya kariya ivaḷ kūntalil malar cūṭṭiṉāy:
erimaruḷ vēṅki yirunta tōki
iḻiyaṇi maṭantiyil tōṉṟum nāṭa

iṉitucey taṉiyāl nunti vāḻiyar
naṉmaṉi vatuvi ayaravivaḷ
piṉṉiruṅkūntal malaraṇin tōyē	(aiṅ. 296)
eṉṟu maṇañceytu koṇṭa kaḷavoḻukkat talivaṉip pārttut tōḻi maṉamāra vāḻttukiṉṟāḷ. kūntalip piṉṉi malaraṇintāṉ eṉṟu kūṟāmal, piṉṉiya kūntalil malaraṇintāṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatāl, piṉṉiyanili kumarik kōlam eṉṟum malaraṇiki kaṟpukkōlam eṉṟum teḷivātal kāṇka. malaraṇi caṭaṅku ceyyumpōtu, taliviyiṉ kūntalit talivaṉ ki toṭum. taṉ kūntalit toṭṭāṉukkuk kumari urimiyākiṉṟāḷ. uṟṟārkku uriyar poṟṟoṭi makaḷir eṉpatu paḻamoḻi. ivaḷ olimeṉ kūntal uriyavām niṉakkē (kuṟun. 225). kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir nāṟiruṅ kūntaṟkiḻavar (puṟam. 113) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl, kūntalukku uriyavaṉ yāvaṉ; avaṉ koḻunaṉ eṉṟa maṇavaḻakkup peṟappaṭum. kūntalukku uriyavaṉ eṉṟāl, kūntalil malar vēyntu maṇaṅkoḷpavaṉ eṉpatu kuṟippu.
nallāvūr kiḻār paṇṭu naṭanta maṇamuṟikaḷit tām pāṭiya orē oru pāṭṭil tokuttuc colluvar. kuḻantikaḷ peṟṟa illaṟa mātar oruṅkukūṭi, kaṟpu vaḻāmal aṟampala ceyka, koṇṭāṉip pēṇi oḻukuka, takka vāḻkkit tuṇiyākuka eṉṟu tirumaṇak kālattu maṇappeṇṇi vāḻttuvar eṉavum, kūntalmēl pūvum piṟavum viḷaṅka naṉmaṇam nikaḻttuvar eṉavum appāṭṭu maṇaccaṭaṅkic collic celkiṉṟatu:-
nīroṭu corinta īritaḻ alari
palliruṅ katuppiṉa nelloṭu tayaṅka
vatuvi naṉmaṇam kaḻinta piṉṟi	(akam. 86)
tirumaṇan toṭṭu makaḷir pūvaṇivar eṉpataṟku ip pāṭṭum kuṟippiṟ cāṉṟātal kāṇka;  im muṭipi niṉittu kaḷavut tuṟip pāṭalkaḷik kaṟkumpōtu, etirccāṉṟukaḷ pōlvaṉa cila tōṉṟakkūṭum; kumarik kālattup pūccūṭum vaḻakkam uṇṭupōlum, atu camutāya vilakkaṉṟu eṉṟu kāṭṭuvatupōlac cila ceyyuṭkaḷ kāṇappaṭum.
vaṇṭuvaḻip paṭarā taṇmalar vēyntu    	(akam. 198)
vīpey kūntal vīcuvaḷi uḷara	(naṟ. 264)
aṇimalar muṇṭakat tāypūṅ kōti
maṇimaruḷ aimpāl vaṇṭupaṭat tiit
tuṇinīrp puvam tuṇiyō ṭāṭi	(naṟ. 265)
kuvaḷi nāṟuṅ kuviyiruṅ kūntal 	(kuṟun. 300)
ivvāṟu varum pakutikaḷik kaṇṭu, eṉ muṭivukku māṟāṉa cāṉṟukaḷ eṉṟu karutiviṭa vēṇṭā. tuṟikaḷōṭu porutti nērpaṭa ārāyiṉ, iyaṟkip puṇarcciyilum uṭaṉpōkkilum iṉpakkaḷippilum iṉpa etirpārppilum nīr viḷiyāṭṭilum kumariyar pūvaṇinta kuṟippuk kaḷākavē irukkum.
muṭipukaḷ
mēṟ ceyta ārāycciyāl kaṇṭa teḷivu eṉṉa? maṇanta kumariyari maṇavāk kumariyariṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭum nōkkamē karaṇat tōṟṟattiṟkuk kāraṇam ām; kāliṟ kiṭanta cilampi akaṟṟuvatu oru karaṇam. kūntal mēl malaraṇivatu oru karaṇam. ivviraṇṭum paṇṭit tirumaṇak kālattup paravalāka vaḻakkil irunta karaṇaṅkaḷ eṉṟu teḷikiṉṟōm. ivaḷ maṉiviyāṉāḷ eṉṟa aṟaniliyic cilampu kaḻitta aṭiyālum, malar vēynta muṭiyālum aṟintu koḷḷalām; cilampu iṉmiyi neruṅkik kāṇavēṇṭum eṉpatilli. cevikkuc cilampiṉ ōci kēḷāmiyāl, ivaḷ maṉivimi eytiyavaḷ eṉpataṉic cēymiyilēyē viḷaṅkik koḷḷalām. malarcūṭiyiruppati aṇukik kāṇa vēṇṭuvatilli; mūkkiṟkup pūmaṇam vantu tākkutalāl, ivaḷ illaṟatti eṉṟu cēymik kaṇṇēyē muṭivu koḷḷalām. aṭiyālum muṭiyālum iṉmik kuṟiyālum kaṉṉimi kaḻinta maṉiyāṭṭi eṉpataṉic tamiḻc camutāyam ellāriyum aṟiyac ceytatu. maṇamāki yāḷit tōṟṟattāl kumariyeṉa mayaṅki viṭātavāṟum, aṅṅaṉam mayaṅkip poyyum vaḻuvum mēṟkoḷḷāta vāṟum karaṇat taṭuppuk kaṇṭatu tamiḻc camutāyam. caṭaṅku tōṉṟak kāraṇam eṉṉa? caṭaṅku eṉṉa? caṭaṅkiṉ kuṟikkōḷ eṉṉa? ivaṟṟiṟku iṉṟu nām viṭi palavāṟu kūṟalām. tol kāppiyam caṅkap paṉuvalkaḷiṉ tuṇikoṇṭu, tamiḻc camutāyam kāṭṭum viṭikaḷi mēlē kaṇṭōm.
caṭaṅku eṉpatu peṇ iṉattiṟku maṭṭumā? maṇamākiya kumaraṉi maṇamākāk kumaraṉiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭa vēṇṭiyatilliyā? ivaṉ tirumaṇattāṉ eṉpataṟku aṭiyāḷam vēṇṭāvā? ivviṉākkaḷukku aṟivu oppum nēriya maṟumoḻi taramuṭiyātutāṉ. kaṟpu irupālārkkum potu eṉṟu nām iṉṟu koḷvatu pōlap paṇṭit tamiḻc camutāyam koḷḷāmalilli; koṇṭiruntatu. eṉiṉum vaḻivaḻippaṭṭa cila ceyalmuṟikaḷi naṭi muṟiyil oruvāṟu oppukkoṇṭu kuṭumpa viyalip pōṟṟiyatu; pōṟṟum poṟuppic ceṟivum niṟivum mikka peṇṇiṉattukku aḷittatu. palatāra maṇam āṭavarkku vaḻakkāṟāka iruntamiyiṉ, ivaṉ maṇaṅkoṇṭāṉ eṉa aṭiyāḷam vitikkavēṇṭum eṉṟa nili tōṉṟavilli. tītilā vaṭamīṉiṉ tiṟam ivaḷ tiṟam eṉṟu kaṇṇakiyiṉ kaṟpiṉ māṭciyip pārāṭṭiṉār iḷaṅkō. kōvalaṉip pārāṭṭumpōtu avar pārāṭṭiyateṉṉa?
maṇtēytta pukaḻiṉāṉ matimuka maṭavārtam
paṇtēytta moḻiyiṉār āyattup pārāṭṭik
kaṇṭēttum cevvēḷeṉ ṟicipōkkik kātalāṟ
koṇṭēttum kiḻamiyāṉ kōvalaṉ
oruvaṉ maṇamākāp peṇṭir palarik kātalikkalām; oruvaṉi maṇamākāp peṇṭir palarum kātalikkalām. ivi tamiḻc camutāyattiṉ kuṟikkōḷ alla. oruvaṉ orutti eṉpatuvē ataṉ kuṟikkōḷ. eṉiṉum oruvaṉ palar eṉṉum naṭimuṟikku iṇaṅkic camutāyam iyaṅka vēṇṭiya tāyiṟṟu. ataṉālaṉṟō piṟaṉmaṉi nayavāmi eṉṟa aṟam pōlap piṟaṉ kaṇavaṉi nayavāmi eṉṟa aṟam tōṉṟa iṭamilli.
kaḷavu kaṟpu eṉṉum kātal nilikaḷum, veṟiyāṭṭu alar uṭaṉpōkku karaṇam mutalāṉa vaḻakkāṟukaḷum tamiḻc camutāyattil kāṇappaṭṭavi. ivaṟṟiyē akavilakkiyat tilum kāṇkiṉṟōm. ākavē akattiṇiyiyal kaṇkaṇṭa camutāya aṭippaṭiyuṭiyatu. naṭimuṟi taḻuviyatu, meyyāṉatu eṉṟu uṇarvōmāka. illatu eṉṟu nāṭṭa eḻutiṉār iṟiyaṉār akapporuḷ uriyāciriyar (pa. 30) akattiṇi poyk kūṟukaḷiṉ puṉivā? ellām puṉineṟi vaḻakkā? illi eṉpar tolkāppiya uriyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar (tol. 999). tamiḻc camutāyattil iruntālum, takāta marapukaḷukku akattiṇiyil iṭamilli? takka marapukaḷukku mikka iṭamuṇṭu. itu poyyeṉap paṭātu, aṟivuṭik kaṟpaṉiyākum. itaṉit tolkāppiyar pulaṉneṟi vaḻakku eṉṟu teḷivākak kūṟuvar. iṟiyaṉārakap poruḷum ataṉ uriyum avaṟṟiṉ vaḻivanta karutturikaḷum akattiṇikku nēr muraṇāka eḻutiya iṭaṅkaḷ pala. poyyāp pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkip poynneṟi vaḻakku (illatu) eṉṟu eḻutiya ivviṭam avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu.

XII      
2. nila aṭippaṭi
akattiṇi amippu uruvāvataṟku utaviya ētukkaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ, mutalāvatākiya camutāya aṭippaṭiyi itu kāṟum ārāyntōm. aṭuttu iṉi ārāyattakkatu puṟanili eṉṉum nila aṭippaṭiyākum. ivaṇkūṟum karuttukkaḷic camutāya aṭippaṭikkum koḷḷalām. ēṉ? evvaḻiyāṉ varum cūḻnilikaḷum ōṭipparavi muṭivil oṉṟāyk kalakkumiṭam camutāyak kaṭalaṉṟō? eṉiṉum, aṅṅaṉam kalanta kūṟukaḷi - kūṟukaḷuḷ nilaccārpālum kālac cārpālum akattiṇi peṟṟa iyaṟkic cāyalkaḷit taṉitteṭuttuk kāṇpōm.
